Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Wingman status notifications in agent terminal

## Context

Wingman status messages (review started, review pending) currently go to stderr, which is invisible in Claude Code's UI. The only visible wingman output is the `additionalContext` prompt injection when REVIEW.md is applied. The user wants informational `systemMessage` notifications so they can see what wingman is doing — not injected into the agent's context, just displayed in the terminal.

## Changes

### 1. Add `outputHookMessage` helper (`cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go`)

New function alongside the existing `outputHookResponse` and `outputHookResponseWithContextAndMessage`. Outputs a JSON hook response with only `systemMessage` — no `additionalContext`, so nothing gets injected into the agent's conversation.

```go
func outputHookMessage(message string) error {
    resp := hookResponse{SystemMessage: message}
    return json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp)
}
```

### 2. Stop hook: wingman status notification (`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`)

Add `outputWingmanStopNotification(repoRoot string)` that checks wingman state and outputs a message. Call it at the end of `commitWithMetadata()` in both:
- The no-changes early return path (~line 297, before `return nil`)
- The normal return path (~line 448, before `return nil`)

Logic:
- If wingman not enabled or `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` set → skip
- If lock file (`.entire/wingman.lock`) exists → review is running → `"[Wingman] Reviewing your changes..."`
- Else if REVIEW.md exists → review done, pending delivery → `"[Wingman] Review pending — will be addressed on your next prompt"`
- Otherwise → no message

The stop hook currently never writes to stdout. Adding a `systemMessage`-only JSON response is safe since Claude Code parses any JSON output from hooks.

### 3. Prompt-submit hook: review-in-progress notification (`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`)

In `captureInitialState()`, the lock file check (lines 103-110) currently writes `"[wingman] Review in progress..."` to stderr only. Change to use `outputHookMessage` so the message appears in the agent terminal.

This runs only when there's NO REVIEW.md (the injection path returns early on line 99). Session initialization runs after and only touches disk/stderr, so outputting JSON before it is safe — no double-write risk.

## Files to modify

- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go` — Add `outputHookMessage` helper
- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — Add stop hook notification function, update prompt-submit lock file message

## Verification

1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`
2. Manual: enable wingman, make changes, trigger stop hook — should see "[Wingman] Reviewing..." in agent terminal
3. Manual: with pending REVIEW.md, trigger stop hook — should see "[Wingman] Review pending..."
4. Manual: with lock file present, send a prompt — should see "[Wingman] Review in progress..."

```
   163→// then delegates to strategy.ExtractInProgressTodo for the actual parsing logic.

   241→// handleSessionStartCommon is the shared implementation for session start hooks.
   242→// Used by both Claude Code and Gemini CLI handlers.
   243→func handleSessionStartCommon() error {
   244→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   255→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
   256→		slog.String("hook", "session-start"),
   257→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   258→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   259→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   260→	)
   261→
   262→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   263→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Build informational message
   267→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
   268→
   269→	// Append wingman note if enabled
   270→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   271→		message += "\n  Wingman is active: your changes will be automatically reviewed."
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
   275→	strat := GetStrategy()
   276→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
   277→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(input.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
   278→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
   279→		}
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Output informational message using agent-specific format
   283→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
   284→		return err
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
   288→	// This handles ENDED → IDLE (re-entering a session).
   289→	// TODO(ENT-221): dispatch ActionWarnStaleSession for ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions.
   290→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(input.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
   291→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
   292→	} else if state != nil {
   293→		strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{})
   294→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   295→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	return nil
   300→}
   301→
   302→// hookSpecificOutput contains event-specific fields nested under hookSpecificOutput
   303→// in the hook response JSON. Claude Code requires this nesting for additionalContext
   304→// to be injected into the agent's conversation.
   305→type hookSpecificOutput struct {
   306→	HookEventName     string `json:"hookEventName"`
   307→	AdditionalContext string `json:"additionalContext,omitempty"`
   308→}
   309→
   310→// hookResponse represents a JSON response to a Claude Code hook.
   311→// systemMessage is shown to the user as a warning/info message.
   312→// hookSpecificOutput contains event-specific fields like additionalContext.
   313→type hookResponse struct {
   314→	SystemMessage      string              `json:"systemMessage,omitempty"`
   315→	HookSpecificOutput *hookSpecificOutput `json:"hookSpecificOutput,omitempty"`
   316→}
   317→
   318→// outputHookResponse outputs a JSON response with additionalContext for
   319→// SessionStart hooks. The context is injected into the agent's conversation.
   320→func outputHookResponse(context string) error {
   321→	resp := hookResponse{
   322→		SystemMessage: context,
   323→		HookSpecificOutput: &hookSpecificOutput{
   324→			HookEventName:     "SessionStart",
   325→			AdditionalContext: context,
   326→		},
   327→	}
   328→	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
   329→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
   330→	}
   331→	return nil
   332→}
   333→
   334→// outputHookResponseWithContextAndMessage outputs a JSON response with both
   335→// additionalContext (injected into agent conversation) and a systemMessage
   336→// (shown to the user as a warning/info).
   337→func outputHookResponseWithContextAndMessage(context, message string) error {
   338→	resp := hookResponse{
   339→		SystemMessage: message,
   340→		HookSpecificOutput: &hookSpecificOutput{
   341→			HookEventName:     "UserPromptSubmit",
   342→			AdditionalContext: context,
   343→		},
   344→	}
   345→	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
   346→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→	return nil
   349→}
```

```
     1→// hooks_claudecode_handlers.go contains Claude Code specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"encoding/json"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    19→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    25→type hookInputData struct {
    26→	agent     agent.Agent
    27→	input     *agent.HookInput
    28→	sessionID string
    29→}
    30→
    31→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    32→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    33→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    34→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    40→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    46→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    47→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    48→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    49→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    50→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    51→	)
    52→
    53→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    54→	if sessionID == "" {
    55→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    59→	return &hookInputData{
    60→		agent:     ag,
    61→		input:     input,
    62→		sessionID: sessionID,
    63→	}, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    67→func captureInitialState() error {
    68→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    69→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return err
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    75→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    76→		return err
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// If a wingman review is pending, inject it as additionalContext so the
    80→	// agent addresses it BEFORE the user's request. This is the primary
    81→	// delivery mechanism — the agent sees the instruction as mandatory context.
    82→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    83→		repoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()
    84→		if rootErr == nil {
    85→			wingmanLogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
    86→			if _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {
    87→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md — injecting into context\n")
    88→				logging.Info(wingmanLogCtx, "wingman injecting review instruction on prompt-submit",
    89→					slog.String("session_id", hookData.sessionID),
    90→				)
    91→				if err := outputHookResponseWithContextAndMessage(
    92→					wingmanApplyInstruction,
    93→					"[Wingman] A code review is pending and will be addressed before your request.",
    94→				); err != nil {
    95→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\n", err)
    96→				} else {
    97→					// Hook response written to stdout — must return immediately
    98→					// to avoid corrupting the JSON with additional output.
    99→					return nil
   100→				}
   101→			}
   102→
   103→			// Log if a review is currently in progress (lock file exists)
   104→			lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   105→			if _, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath); statErr == nil {
   106→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review in progress...\n")
   107→				logging.Info(wingmanLogCtx, "wingman review in progress",
   108→					slog.String("session_id", hookData.sessionID),
   109→				)
   110→			}
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   115→	strat := GetStrategy()
   116→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   117→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
   118→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
   119→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   120→		}
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	return nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
   127→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
   128→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   129→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   135→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   136→	if err != nil {
   137→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   141→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   142→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   143→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   144→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   145→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   146→	)
   147→
   148→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   149→	if sessionID == "" {
   150→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   154→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   159→	// Without this, the function does a lot of work (transcript copy, prompt extraction, etc.)
   160→	// before eventually failing deep in strategy.SaveChanges().
   161→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   162→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   163→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   167→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   168→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   169→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   172→	}
   173→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   178→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   179→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   180→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   181→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   182→
   183→	// Copy transcript
   184→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   185→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   189→
   190→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   191→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   192→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   198→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   199→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   200→	var transcriptOffset int
   201→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   202→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   203→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   204→	} else {
   205→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   206→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   207→		if loadErr != nil {
   208→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   209→		}
   210→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   211→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   212→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   213→		}
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   217→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   218→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   219→	var totalLines int
   220→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   221→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   222→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   223→		if err != nil {
   224→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   225→		}
   226→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   227→	} else {
   228→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   229→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   230→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   231→		if err != nil {
   232→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   233→		}
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   237→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   238→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   239→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   240→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   241→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   242→	}
   243→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   244→
   245→	// Extract summary
   246→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   247→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   248→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   249→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   250→	}
   251→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   252→
   253→	// Get modified files from transcript
   254→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   255→
   256→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   257→	lastPrompt := ""
   258→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   259→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   260→	}
   261→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   262→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   263→
   264→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   265→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   266→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   267→	if err != nil {
   268→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	if preState != nil {
   272→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   276→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   277→	if err != nil {
   278→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   282→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   283→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   284→	if changes != nil {
   285→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   286→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   290→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   291→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   292→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   293→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   294→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   295→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   296→		// Auto-apply pending wingman review even when no file changes this turn
   297→		triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot)
   298→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   299→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   300→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   301→		}
   302→		return nil
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   306→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   307→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   308→	}
   309→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   310→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   311→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   312→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   313→		}
   314→	}
   315→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   316→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   317→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   318→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   319→		}
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	// Create context file before saving changes
   323→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   324→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   325→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   326→	}
   327→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   328→
   329→	// Get git author from local/global config
   330→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	// Get the configured strategy
   336→	strat := GetStrategy()
   337→
   338→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   339→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   340→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   344→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   345→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   346→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   350→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   351→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   352→	if preState != nil {
   353→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   354→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   358→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   359→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   360→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   361→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   362→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   363→		if err != nil {
   364→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   365→		} else {
   366→			tokenUsage = usage
   367→		}
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   371→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   372→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   373→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   374→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   375→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   376→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   377→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   378→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   379→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   380→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   381→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   382→		AgentType:                agentType,
   383→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   384→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   385→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   389→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   393→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   394→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   395→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   396→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   397→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   398→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   399→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   400→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   401→		if loadErr != nil {
   402→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   403→		}
   404→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   405→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   406→		if sessionState == nil {
   407→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   408→				SessionID: sessionID,
   409→			}
   410→		}
   411→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   412→		sessionState.StepCount++
   413→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   414→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   415→		} else {
   416→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   417→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   418→		}
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   422→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   423→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   424→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   425→
   426→	// Trigger wingman review for auto-commit strategy (commit already happened
   427→	// in SaveChanges). Manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook
   428→	// instead, since the user commits manually.
   429→	if totalChanges > 0 && strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   430→		triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   431→			SessionID:     sessionID,
   432→			RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   433→			ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,
   434→			NewFiles:      relNewFiles,
   435→			DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,
   436→			Prompts:       allPrompts,
   437→			CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   438→		})
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Auto-apply pending wingman review on turn end
   442→	triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot)
   443→
   444→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   445→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   446→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   447→	}
   448→
   449→	return nil
   450→}
   451→
   452→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   453→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   454→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   455→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   456→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	// Get agent for logging context
   462→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   463→	if err != nil {
   464→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   468→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   469→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   470→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   471→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   472→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   473→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   474→	)
   475→
   476→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   477→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   478→	if !found {
   479→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   480→		return nil
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   484→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   485→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   486→		return nil
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   490→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   491→	if err != nil {
   492→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   493→		return nil
   494→	}
   495→
   496→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   497→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   498→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   499→		return nil
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	// Get git author
   503→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   504→	if err != nil {
   505→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   506→		return nil
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Get the active strategy
   510→	strat := GetStrategy()
   511→
   512→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   513→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   514→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   515→		return nil
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   519→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   520→	if sessionID == "" {
   521→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   525→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   526→
   527→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   528→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   529→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   530→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   531→	if todoContent == "" {
   532→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   533→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   534→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   535→		if todoCount > 0 {
   536→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   537→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   538→		}
   539→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   540→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   544→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   545→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   546→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   547→	}
   548→
   549→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   550→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   551→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   552→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   553→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   554→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   555→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   556→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   557→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   558→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   559→		IsIncremental:       true,
   560→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   561→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   562→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   563→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   564→		AgentType:           agentType,
   565→	}
   566→
   567→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   568→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   569→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   570→		return nil
   571→	}
   572→
   573→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   574→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   575→	return nil
   576→}
   577→
   578→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   579→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   580→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   581→	if err != nil {
   582→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Get agent for logging context
   586→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   587→	if err != nil {
   588→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   589→	}
   590→
   591→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   592→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   593→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   594→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   595→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   596→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   597→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   598→	)
   599→
   600→	// Log context to stdout
   601→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   602→
   603→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   604→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   605→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   606→	// in file changes.
   607→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   608→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	return nil
   612→}
   613→
   614→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   615→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   616→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   617→	if err != nil {
   618→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   622→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   623→
   624→	// Get agent for logging context
   625→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   626→	if err != nil {
   627→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	// Log parsed input context
   631→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   632→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   633→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   634→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   635→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   636→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   637→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   638→	)
   639→
   640→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   641→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   642→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   643→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   644→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   645→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   646→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   647→		}
   648→	}
   649→
   650→	// Log context to stdout
   651→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   652→
   653→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   654→	var modifiedFiles []string
   655→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   656→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   657→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   658→		if err != nil {
   659→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   660→		} else {
   661→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   662→		}
   663→	} else {
   664→		// Fall back to main transcript
   665→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   666→		if err != nil {
   667→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   668→		} else {
   669→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   670→		}
   671→	}
   672→
   673→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   674→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   675→	if err != nil {
   676→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   677→	}
   678→
   679→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   680→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   681→	if err != nil {
   682→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   686→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   687→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   688→	if err != nil {
   689→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	// Filter and normalize paths
   693→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   694→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   695→	if changes != nil {
   696→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   697→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   701→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   702→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   703→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   704→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   705→		return nil
   706→	}
   707→
   708→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   709→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   710→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   711→
   712→	// Get git author
   713→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   714→	if err != nil {
   715→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	// Get the configured strategy
   719→	strat := GetStrategy()
   720→
   721→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   722→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   723→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   724→	}
   725→
   726→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   727→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   728→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   729→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   730→	}
   731→
   732→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   733→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   734→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   735→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   736→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   737→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   738→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   739→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   740→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   741→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   742→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   743→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   744→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   745→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   746→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   747→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   748→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   749→		AgentType:              agentType,
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   753→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   754→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   755→	}
   756→
   757→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   758→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   759→
   760→	return nil
   761→}
   762→
   763→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   764→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   765→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   766→}
   767→
   768→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   769→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   770→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   771→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   772→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   773→	if err != nil {
   774→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   775→	}
   776→
   777→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   778→	if err != nil {
   779→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   783→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   784→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   785→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   786→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   787→	)
   788→
   789→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   790→		return nil // No session to update
   791→	}
   792→
   793→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   794→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   795→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   796→	}
   797→
   798→	// If a wingman review is pending and this was the last live session,
   799→	// trigger background auto-apply. No more prompts will come from this
   800→	// session, so the prompt-submit injection can't deliver the review.
   801→	triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd()
   802→
   803→	return nil
   804→}
   805→
   806→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   807→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   808→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   809→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   810→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   811→	if loadErr != nil {
   812→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   813→		return
   814→	}
   815→	if turnState == nil {
   816→		return
   817→	}
   818→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   819→
   820→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   821→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   822→		strat := GetStrategy()
   823→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   824→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   825→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   826→			}
   827→		}
   828→	}
   829→
   830→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   831→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   832→	}
   833→}
   834→
   835→// triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending checks for a pending REVIEW.md and spawns
   836→// the auto-apply subprocess if conditions are met. Called from the stop hook
   837→// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).
   838→//
   839→// When a live session exists, this is a no-op: the prompt-submit injection
   840→// will deliver the review visibly in the user's terminal instead. Background
   841→// auto-apply is only used when no sessions are alive (all ended).
   842→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
   843→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   844→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   845→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply skip: wingman not enabled")
   846→		return
   847→	}
   848→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   849→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply skip: already in apply subprocess")
   850→		return
   851→	}
   852→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   853→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
   854→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply skip: no REVIEW.md pending")
   855→		return
   856→	}
   857→	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   858→	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   859→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping",
   860→			slog.Time("attempted_at", *wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt),
   861→		)
   862→		return
   863→	}
   864→	// Don't spawn background auto-apply if a live session exists.
   865→	// The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as additionalContext,
   866→	// which is visible to the user in their terminal.
   867→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   868→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred: live session will handle via injection")
   869→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review pending — will be injected on next prompt\n")
   870→		return
   871→	}
   872→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply (no live sessions)\n")
   873→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning (no live sessions)",
   874→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
   875→	)
   876→	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
   877→}
   878→
   879→// triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd checks for a pending REVIEW.md after a
   880→// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.
   881→// Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd hooks.
   882→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd() {
   883→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   884→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   885→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end skip: wingman not enabled")
   886→		return
   887→	}
   888→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   889→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end skip: already in apply subprocess")
   890→		return
   891→	}
   892→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   893→	if err != nil {
   894→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end skip: cannot resolve repo root")
   895→		return
   896→	}
   897→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   898→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
   899→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end skip: no REVIEW.md pending")
   900→		return
   901→	}
   902→	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   903→	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   904→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end skip: already attempted",
   905→			slog.Time("attempted_at", *wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt),
   906→		)
   907→		return
   908→	}
   909→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   910→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred on session-end: other live sessions remain")
   911→		return
   912→	}
   913→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Last session ended with pending review, spawning auto-apply\n")
   914→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end (no live sessions remain)",
   915→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
   916→	)
   917→	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
   918→}
   919→
   920→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   921→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   922→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   923→	if err != nil {
   924→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   925→	}
   926→	if state == nil {
   927→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   928→	}
   929→
   930→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   931→
   932→	now := time.Now()
   933→	state.EndedAt = &now
   934→
   935→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   936→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   937→	}
   938→	return nil
   939→}
   940→
   941→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   942→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   943→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   944→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   945→
   946→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   947→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   948→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   949→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   950→//
   951→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   952→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   953→//
   954→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   955→// with whatever data is available.
   956→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   957→	const (
   958→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   959→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   960→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   961→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   962→	)
   963→
   964→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   965→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   966→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   967→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   968→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   969→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   970→			)
   971→			return
   972→		}
   973→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   974→	}
   975→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   976→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   977→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   978→	)
   979→}
   980→
   981→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   982→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   983→// close to hookStartTime.
   984→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   985→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   986→	if err != nil {
   987→		return false
   988→	}
   989→	defer f.Close()
   990→
   991→	// Seek to tail
   992→	info, err := f.Stat()
   993→	if err != nil {
   994→		return false
   995→	}
   996→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   997→	if offset < 0 {
   998→		offset = 0
   999→	}
  1000→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
  1001→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
  1002→		return false
  1003→	}
  1004→
  1005→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
  1006→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
  1007→	for _, line := range lines {
  1008→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1009→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
  1010→			continue
  1011→		}
  1012→
  1013→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
  1014→		var entry struct {
  1015→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
  1016→		}
  1017→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
  1018→			continue
  1019→		}
  1020→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
  1021→		if err != nil {
  1022→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
  1023→			if err != nil {
  1024→				continue
  1025→			}
  1026→		}
  1027→
  1028→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
  1029→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
  1030→			return true
  1031→		}
  1032→	}
  1033→	return false
  1034→}
  1035→
```

This makes wingman activity visible\n// in the agent terminal without injecting context into the agent's conversation.\nfunc outputWingmanStopNotification(repoRoot string) {\n\tif !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") != \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(lockPath); err == nil {\n\t\t_ = outputHookMessage(\"[Wingman] Reviewing your changes...\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\t_ = outputHookMessage(\"[Wingman] Review pending \\u2014 will be addressed on your next prompt\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n}\n\n// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from"
```json
}
```

> AGENT

All changes implemented and verified. Here's a summary:

**`cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go`** — Added `outputHookMessage` helper that outputs a JSON hook response with only `systemMessage` (no `additionalContext`), so the message is visible in the agent terminal but not injected into the agent's conversation.

**`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`** — Three changes:

1. **New `outputWingmanStopNotification` function** — checks wingman state at stop hook time:
   - Lock file present → `"[Wingman] Reviewing your changes..."`
   - REVIEW.md present → `"[Wingman] Review pending — will be addressed on your next prompt"`
   
2. **Called at both return paths in `commitWithMetadata`** — the no-changes early return and the normal return, so the notification fires regardless of whether files were changed.

3. **Prompt-submit lock file message** — changed from `fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, ...)` to `outputHookMessage(...)` so the `"[Wingman] Review in progress..."` message is visible in the agent terminal instead of only going to stderr.

```
+// to the user in the terminal but NOT injected into the agent's conversation.
+// Use this for informational notifications (e.g., wingman status) that the user
+// should see but the agent should not act on.
+func outputHookMessage(message string) error {
+	resp := hookResponse{SystemMessage: message}
+	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // outputHookResponseWithContextAndMessage outputs a JSON response with both
 // additionalContext (injected into agent conversation) and a systemMessage
 // (shown to the user as a warning/info).

@@ -100,13 +100,17 @@ func captureInitialState() error {
 				}
 			}
 
-			// Log if a review is currently in progress (lock file exists)
+			// Notify if a review is currently in progress (lock file exists).
+			// outputHookMessage writes JSON to stdout; session initialization
+			// below only touches disk/stderr, so there's no double-write risk.
 			lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
 			if _, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath); statErr == nil {
-				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review in progress...\n")
 				logging.Info(wingmanLogCtx, "wingman review in progress",
 					slog.String("session_id", hookData.sessionID),
 				)
+				if err := outputHookMessage("[Wingman] Review in progress..."); err != nil {
+					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to output review-in-progress message: %v\n", err)
+				}
 			}
 		}
 	}
@@ -299,6 +303,7 @@ func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codeba
 		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
 			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
 		}
+		outputWingmanStopNotification(repoRoot)
 		return nil
 	}
 
@@ -446,6 +451,8 @@ func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codeba

@@ -803,6 +810,30 @@ func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {

+// outputWingmanStopNotification outputs a systemMessage notification about
+// wingman status at the end of a stop hook. This makes wingman activity visible
+// in the agent terminal without injecting context into the agent's conversation.
+func outputWingmanStopNotification(repoRoot string) {
+	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
+		return
+	}
+	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
+		return
+	}
+
+	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
+	if _, err := os.Stat(lockPath); err == nil {
+		_ = outputHookMessage("[Wingman] Reviewing your changes...") //nolint:errcheck // best-effort notification
+		return
+	}
+
+	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
+	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
+		_ = outputHookMessage("[Wingman] Review pending \u2014 will be addressed on your next prompt") //nolint:errcheck // best-effort notification
+		return
+	}
+}
+

 // on failure rather than returning errors.
```

     1→# Code Review
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→This change successfully implements user-visible wingman status notifications in the agent terminal. The implementation adds a new `outputHookMessage` helper that emits JSON responses with only `systemMessage` (no `additionalContext`), making wingman activity visible without injecting context into the agent's conversation. The changes are well-structured and include comprehensive test coverage.
     5→
     6→## Issues
     7→
     8→### [WARNING] Potential infinite recursion in triggerWingmanFromCommit
     9→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go:346`
    10→**Description:** The `triggerWingmanFromCommit` function checks `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` to prevent recursion, but the environment variable is only set in `triggerAutoApply` (called from the review subprocess). If `triggerWingmanFromCommit` is called from the post-commit hook during a manual-commit strategy workflow, and that commit happens during a non-apply wingman operation, the recursion guard won't trigger. The comment says "manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook" but there's no guard against the review subprocess itself triggering another post-commit hook if it makes a commit.
    11→**Suggestion:**
    12→```go
    13→// In triggerWingmanFromCommit, add an additional guard:
    14→if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_REVIEW") != "" {
    15→    return
    16→}
    17→
    18→// Then in spawnDetachedWingmanReview, set the env var:
    19→env := append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_REVIEW=1")
    20→cmd.Env = env
    21→```
    22→
    23→### [SUGGESTION] outputWingmanStopNotification may output stale status
    24→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:815`
    25→**Description:** `outputWingmanStopNotification` checks for lock file and review file existence at the end of the stop hook, but these files may have been modified by concurrent wingman processes between the check and the output. The notification could say "Review pending" when the review was just applied, or "Reviewing..." when the review just finished.
    26→**Suggestion:**
    27→Consider adding timestamps or version checks, or accept this as best-effort status reporting. For a best-effort notification, the current implementation is reasonable, but a comment acknowledging the race would help future maintainers:
    28→```go
    29→// outputWingmanStopNotification outputs a systemMessage notification about
    30→// wingman status at the end of a stop hook. This is best-effort — status
    31→// may change between check and display due to concurrent wingman processes.
    32→```
    33→
    34→### [SUGGESTION] Missing error handling in notification outputs
    35→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:456` and `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:831`
    36→**Description:** Several calls to `outputHookMessage` use `//nolint:errcheck` with comment "best-effort notification", but if the JSON output is malformed or stdout is closed, the hook response could be corrupted and break Claude Code's hook parsing. While notifications are best-effort, corrupting the hook response is worse than silently failing.
    37→**Suggestion:**
    38→```go
    39→// If outputHookMessage fails, log to stderr but don't corrupt hook output
    40→if err := outputHookMessage("[Wingman] Review in progress..."); err != nil {
    41→    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to output status message: %v\n", err)
    42→}
    43→```
    44→
    45→### [SUGGESTION] fileExists helper not defined
    46→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:433`
    47→**Description:** The code calls `fileExists(reviewPath)` but this helper function is not defined in the diff. Either it exists elsewhere in the codebase or this will cause a compilation error.
    48→**Suggestion:**
    49→If the helper doesn't exist, replace with:
    50→```go
    51→if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {
    52→    wingmanLog("no REVIEW.md found at %s, nothing to apply", reviewPath)
    53→    return nil
    54→}
    55→```

```
     1→# Wingman: Automated Code Review
     2→
     3→Wingman is an automated code review system that reviews agent-produced code changes after each commit and delivers actionable suggestions back to the agent.
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→When enabled, wingman runs a background review after each commit (or checkpoint), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and ensures the agent addresses them. The system prioritizes **visible delivery** — the user should see the agent reading and applying the review in their terminal.
     8→
     9→## Architecture
    10→
    11→### Components
    12→
    13→| Component | File | Purpose |
    14→|-----------|------|---------|
    15→| Trigger & state | `wingman.go` | Payload, state management, dedup, lock files, stale review cleanup |
    16→| Review process | `wingman_review.go` | Detached subprocess: diff, Claude review call, REVIEW.md creation, session detection |
    17→| Review prompt | `wingman_prompt.go` | Builds the review prompt from diff, prompts, context |
    18→| Instruction | `wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded instruction injected into agent context |
    19→| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached subprocess spawning (Unix) |
    20→| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stubs (non-Unix) |
    21→| Hook integration | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Prompt-submit injection, stop hook trigger, session-end trigger |
    22→
    23→### State Files
    24→
    25→| File | Purpose |
    26→|------|---------|
    27→| `.entire/REVIEW.md` | The review itself, read by the agent |
    28→| `.entire/wingman-state.json` | Dedup state, session ID, apply tracking |
    29→| `.entire/wingman.lock` | Prevents concurrent review spawns |
    30→| `.entire/wingman-payload.json` | Payload passed to detached review process |
    31→| `.entire/logs/wingman.log` | Review process logs (stderr redirect) |
    32→
    33→## Lifecycle
    34→
    35→### Phase 1: Trigger
    36→
    37→A wingman review is triggered after code changes are committed:
    38→
    39→- **Manual-commit strategy**: Git `post-commit` hook calls `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`
    40→- **Auto-commit strategy**: Stop hook calls `triggerWingmanReview()` after `SaveChanges()`
    41→
    42→Before spawning, preconditions are checked:
    43→1. `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` env var not set (prevents recursion from auto-apply)
    44→2. No pending REVIEW.md for the current session (`shouldSkipPendingReview()`)
    45→3. Lock file acquired atomically (`acquireWingmanLock()`)
    46→4. File hash dedup — skip if same files were already reviewed this session
    47→
    48→### Phase 2: Review (Detached Process)
    49→
    50→The review runs in a fully detached subprocess (`entire wingman __review <payload-path>`):
    51→
    52→```
    53→┌─ Detached Process ─────────────────────────────────────┐
    54→│ 1. Read payload from file                              │
    55→│ 2. Wait 10s for agent turn to settle                   │
    56→│ 3. Compute diff (merge-base with main/master)          │
    57→│ 4. Load session context from checkpoint metadata       │
    58→│ 5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + context)      │
    59→│ 6. Call claude --print (sonnet model, read-only tools)  │
    60→│ 7. Write REVIEW.md                                     │
    61→│ 8. Save dedup state                                    │
    62→│ 9. Determine delivery path (see Phase 3)               │
    63→│ 10. Remove lock file                                   │
    64→└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    65→```
    66→
    67→The review process uses `--setting-sources ""` to disable hooks (prevents recursion) and strips `GIT_*` environment variables for isolation.
    68→
    69→### Phase 3: Delivery
    70→
    71→There are two delivery mechanisms. The system chooses based on whether any session is still alive.
    72→
    73→#### Primary: Prompt-Submit Injection (Visible)
    74→
    75→When a live session exists (IDLE, ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase — excluding stale ACTIVE sessions older than 4 hours), the review is delivered through the agent's next prompt:
    76→
    77→```
    78→Review finishes → REVIEW.md written → live session detected → defer to injection
    79→
    80→User sends prompt → UserPromptSubmit hook fires
    81→                  → REVIEW.md exists on disk
    82→                  → Inject as additionalContext (mandatory agent instruction)
    83→                  → Agent reads REVIEW.md, applies suggestions, deletes file
    84→                  → Agent then proceeds with user's actual request
    85→```
    86→
    87→The `additionalContext` hook response field adds the instruction directly to Claude's context, making it a mandatory pre-step rather than an ignorable warning. The embedded instruction (`wingman_instruction.md`) tells the agent to:
    88→
    89→1. Read `.entire/REVIEW.md`
    90→2. Address each suggestion (skip any it disagrees with)
    91→3. Delete `.entire/REVIEW.md` when done
    92→4. Briefly tell the user what changed
    93→
    94→This path is **visible** — the user sees the agent working through the review in their terminal.
    95→
    96→#### Fallback: Background Auto-Apply (Invisible)
    97→
    98→When no live sessions exist (all ENDED or none), REVIEW.md is applied via a background process:
    99→
   100→```
   101→Review finishes → REVIEW.md written → no live sessions → background auto-apply
   102→
   103→entire wingman __apply <repoRoot>
   104→  → Verify REVIEW.md exists
   105→  → Check ApplyAttemptedAt not set (retry prevention)
   106→  → Re-check session phase is not ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED (guard against race)
   107→  → claude --continue --print --permission-mode acceptEdits
   108→```
   109→
   110→This path is **invisible** — it runs silently. It exists as a fallback for when no session will receive the injection (e.g., user closed all sessions during the review window). Both IDLE and ENDED phases are considered safe for auto-apply — only truly active sessions (ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED) block it.
   111→
   112→### Decision Flow
   113→
   114→```
   115→                    REVIEW.md written
   116→                          │
   117→                          ▼
   118→                ┌─────────────────┐
   119→                │ Any live session │
   120→                │   exists?        │
   121→                └────────┬────────┘
   122→                    │          │
   123→                   Yes         No
   124→                    │          │
   125→                    ▼          ▼
   126→            ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────────┐
   127→            │  Defer   │ │  Background  │
   128→            │  to next │ │  auto-apply  │
   129→            │  prompt  │ │  immediately │
   130→            └──────────┘ └──────────────┘
   131→                    │
   132→                    ▼
   133→            User sends prompt
   134→                    │
   135→                    ▼
   136→            additionalContext
   137→            injection fires
   138→                    │
   139→                    ▼
   140→            Agent applies review
   141→            (visible in terminal)
   142→```
   143→
   144→### Trigger Points Summary
   145→
   146→| Trigger | When | What Happens |
   147→|---------|------|-------------|
   148→| **Review process** (`runWingmanReview`) | Review finishes | If no live sessions → background auto-apply. Otherwise defer. |
   149→| **Prompt-submit hook** (`captureInitialState`) | User sends prompt | If REVIEW.md exists → inject as `additionalContext`. |
   150→| **Stop hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`) | Agent turn ends | If REVIEW.md exists + no live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |
   151→| **Session-end hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd`) | User closes session | If REVIEW.md exists + no remaining live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |
   152→
   153→## Timing
   154→
   155→Typical timeline for a review cycle:
   156→
   157→```
   158→T+0s    Commit happens → wingman review triggered
   159→T+0s    Lock file created, payload written
   160→T+10s   Initial settle delay completes
   161→T+10s   Diff computed (~30-50ms)
   162→T+11s   Claude review API call starts
   163→T+30-50s Review received, REVIEW.md written
   164→T+30-50s Delivery path determined
   165→```
   166→
   167→The 10-second initial delay lets the agent turn fully settle before computing the diff, ensuring all file writes are flushed.
   168→
   169→## Review Prompt Construction
   170→
   171→The review prompt is the core of wingman's value. Unlike a naive diff-only review, it leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built.
   172→
   173→### Context Sources
   174→
   175→The prompt is assembled from six sources, each contributing a different layer of understanding:
   176→
   177→| Source | Origin | What It Provides |
   178→|--------|--------|-----------------|
   179→| **Developer prompts** | `prompt.txt` from checkpoint metadata | The original instructions given to the agent — the ground truth of intent |
   180→| **Commit message** | Git commit or auto-commit message | A summary of what was done (may differ from what was asked) |
   181→| **Session context** | `context.md` from checkpoint metadata | Generated summary of key actions, decisions, and session flow |
   182→| **Checkpoint files** | `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/` | Paths provided so the reviewer can read the full transcript, prompts, or context if needed |
   183→| **File list** | Payload from trigger | Which files changed and how (modified/new/deleted) |
   184→| **Branch diff** | `git diff` against merge-base | The actual code changes — computed against `main`/`master` for a holistic branch-level view |
   185→
   186→### Prompt Structure
   187→
   188→```
   189→┌─ System Role ──────────────────────────────────────────┐
   190→│ "You are a senior code reviewer performing an           │
   191→│  intent-aware review."                                  │
   192→├─ Session Context ──────────────────────────────────────┤
   193→│ Developer's Prompts     <prompts>...</prompts>          │
   194→│ Commit Message          (plain text)                    │
   195→│ Session Context         <session-context>...</session-context> │
   196→│ Checkpoint File Paths   (for deeper investigation)      │
   197→├─ Code Changes ─────────────────────────────────────────┤
   198→│ Files changed:          file.go (modified), ...         │
   199→│ Diff                    <diff>...</diff>                │
   200→├─ Review Instructions ──────────────────────────────────┤
   201→│ Intent alignment        (most important)                │
   202→│ Correctness             bugs, logic errors, races       │
   203→│ Security                injection, secrets, path traversal │
   204→│ Robustness              edge cases, leaks, timeouts     │
   205→│ Do NOT flag             style, docs on clear code       │
   206→│ Output format           Markdown with severity levels   │
   207→└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   208→```
   209→
   210→### Intent-Aware Review
   211→
   212→The review instructions prioritize **intent alignment** as the most important category:
   213→
   214→1. Do the changes actually accomplish what the developer asked for?
   215→2. Are there any prompts or requirements that were missed or only partially implemented?
   216→3. Does the implementation match the stated approach in the session context?
   217→
   218→This is only possible because Entire captures the developer's prompts and session context as checkpoint metadata. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.
   219→
   220→### Diff Strategy
   221→
   222→The diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:
   223→
   224→1. `git merge-base main HEAD` → diff against merge-base (branch-level view)
   225→2. `git merge-base master HEAD` → try master if main doesn't exist
   226→3. `git diff HEAD` → uncommitted changes only
   227→4. `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` → latest commit if no uncommitted changes
   228→
   229→### Read-Only Tool Access
   230→
   231→The reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to:
   232→
   233→- Read source files beyond the diff for additional context
   234→- Search for related code patterns or usages
   235→- Inspect the checkpoint metadata files referenced in the prompt
   236→
   237→Tools are restricted to read-only operations — the reviewer cannot modify files.
   238→
   239→### Truncation
   240→
   241→Diffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. Very large diffs tend to produce unfocused reviews.
   242→
   243→### Output Format
   244→
   245→The reviewer outputs structured Markdown with:
   246→- **Summary**: Does the change accomplish its goal? Overall quality assessment.
   247→- **Issues**: Each with severity (`CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `SUGGESTION`), file path with line reference, description, and suggested fix.
   248→
   249→This output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md` and later read by the agent during delivery.
   250→
   251→## Stale Review Cleanup
   252→
   253→Reviews can become stale in several scenarios. The `shouldSkipPendingReview()` function handles cleanup:
   254→
   255→| Scenario | Detection | Action |
   256→|----------|-----------|--------|
   257→| REVIEW.md without state file | `state == nil` | Delete REVIEW.md (orphan) |
   258→| REVIEW.md from different session | `state.SessionID != currentSessionID` | Delete REVIEW.md (stale) |
   259→| REVIEW.md older than 1 hour | `time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1h` | Delete REVIEW.md (TTL expired) |
   260→| REVIEW.md from current session | Session matches + fresh | Keep (skip new review) |
   261→
   262→## Retry Prevention
   263→
   264→The `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply attempts:
   265→
   266→- Set to current time before triggering auto-apply
   267→- Reset to `nil` when a new review is written
   268→- Checked before every auto-apply attempt — if set, skip
   269→
   270→## Concurrency Safety
   271→
   272→- **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.
   273→- **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.
   274→- **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.
   275→- **GIT_* stripping**: Subprocesses strip all `GIT_*` env vars to prevent index corruption.
   276→- **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).
   277→- **Stale session detection**: ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions not updated in 4+ hours are considered orphaned (crashed agent) and ignored by `hasAnyLiveSession`. IDLE sessions are always considered live regardless of age.
   278→
   279→## Configuration
   280→
   281→```bash
   282→entire wingman enable   # Enable wingman auto-review
   283→entire wingman disable  # Disable and clean up pending reviews
   284→entire wingman status   # Show current status
   285→```
   286→
   287→Wingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:
   288→
   289→```json
   290→{
   291→  "strategy_options": {
   292→    "wingman": {
   293→      "enabled": true
   294→    }
   295→  }
   296→}
   297→```
   298→
   299→## Key Constants
   300→
   301→| Constant | Value | Purpose |
   302→|----------|-------|---------|
   303→| `wingmanInitialDelay` | 10s | Settle time before computing diff |
   304→| `wingmanReviewModel` | `sonnet` | Model used for reviews |
   305→| `wingmanGitTimeout` | 60s | Timeout for git diff operations |
   306→| `wingmanReviewTimeout` | 5m | Timeout for Claude review API call |
   307→| `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |
   308→| `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |
   309→| `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |
   310→| `staleActiveSessionThreshold` | 4h | Max age before ACTIVE session is considered stale/orphaned |
   311→
```
