---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go:65**
The detached spawning functions silently ignore all errors during the spawn process (lines 58-62 for review, 105-109 for apply). If spawning fails (e.g., executable not found, permissions issue, resource exhaustion), the failure is completely invisible to the caller. While this is documented as "best effort" behavior, consider at least logging the failure to stderr so users/operators can diagnose issues. The current implementation makes it impossible to tell whether wingman failed to spawn or is genuinely disabled.

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go:64**
The log file is opened with `os.O_APPEND` but never closed in the spawning function. While the process will be detached and the file descriptor will be closed when the subprocess exits, this creates a temporary leak in the parent process between lines 46-67 if `cmd.Start()` fails. The logFile should be closed in the error path at line 59-60 to prevent the leak.

---
**cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go:325**
The `outputHookResponseWithContext` function uses a parameter named `context` which shadows the imported `context` package. While Go allows this, it can cause confusion and makes it impossible to use the context package within the function without aliasing. Consider renaming the parameter to `additionalContext` or `instruction` to match the field name and avoid shadowing.
```suggestion
func outputHookResponseWithContext(additionalContext string) error {
	resp := hookResponse{
		AdditionalContext: additionalContext,
```

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:211**
The `baseCommit` parameter is unused (indicated by the underscore). This parameter is passed from the payload but ignored in favor of computing the merge-base dynamically. If the baseCommit is not needed, consider removing it from the WingmanPayload struct to simplify the interface. If it was intended to be used as a fallback when merge-base detection fails, the implementation should be updated to use it.

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:237**
When `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` fails, the function returns an empty string with nil error. This silently hides the error from the caller, who won't know whether the diff is genuinely empty or if the git command failed. The error should be returned to allow proper error handling. This could cause the review process to complete successfully with no diff, wasting API calls to Claude.
```suggestion
			return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff for latest commit failed: %w", err)
```

---
**cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go:33**
The Agent field is added to settings but there's no migration or initialization logic shown in the diff. If existing installations have settings files without this field, the mergeJSON function will handle it properly (via the omitempty tag), but there's no clear documentation of when/how this field should be set. Consider documenting the expected usage pattern or ensuring proper initialization during enable/setup.
```suggestion
	// Agent is the name of the coding agent integration (e.g., "claude-code", "amp").
	// This field is optional and may be empty for existing installations that predate
	// the agent setting; in that case, the CLI will use its default/auto-detected
	// agent behavior. It is typically set during Entire enable/setup or by manually
	// editing the settings file.
```

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go:437**
The stale lock cleanup logic at lines 363-377 has a TOCTOU (Time-Of-Check-Time-Of-Use) race condition. Between checking the lock file age and removing/recreating it, another process could have already removed the stale lock and acquired a new lock. The second `OpenFile` call would then fail with `os.ErrExist`, but the error is not properly distinguished from the initial existence check. While the impact is low (just a skipped review), consider using atomic operations or accepting this as a benign race.
```suggestion
		if err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
				// Another process acquired the lock in the meantime; treat as normal contention.
				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
				return false
			}
```

---
**cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:950**
The critical auto-apply trigger functions (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending` and `triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd`) lack unit tests. These functions handle important decision logic about when to spawn background processes and have multiple conditional branches (enabled check, env var check, REVIEW.md existence, ApplyAttemptedAt check, live session check). Consider adding tests to verify the correct behavior under different conditions, especially the retry prevention logic via `ApplyAttemptedAt`.

---
**docs/architecture/wingman.md:67**
The documentation states that the review process "strips `GIT_*` environment variables for isolation" (line 67), but the actual implementation in `wingman_spawn_unix.go` at line 37 sets `cmd.Env = os.Environ()` without stripping GIT_* variables. The stripping only happens in `callClaudeForReview` (line 294 of wingman_review.go) and `triggerAutoApply` (line 541 of wingman_review.go). This means the detached wingman process itself inherits all GIT_* environment variables, which could potentially cause issues if the parent was invoked from a git hook. Update the documentation to accurately reflect when GIT_* stripping occurs.

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:282**
The `wingman --setting-sources ""` flag is used to disable hooks and prevent recursion (line 282). However, if the `claude` CLI command doesn't properly handle an empty string for `--setting-sources`, this could either fail to disable hooks or cause an error. Verify that the Claude CLI correctly interprets `--setting-sources ""` as "disable all setting sources" and doesn't treat it as a malformed argument.
```suggestion
		"--setting-sources", "none",
```

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:367**
The loop enforces a maxSessionCheck limit of 50 but returns false when the limit is hit, which could lead to incorrect behavior. If there are exactly 50 ended sessions and one IDLE session (session #51), the function would incorrectly return false even though an IDLE session exists. Consider either increasing the limit, making it configurable, or logging a warning and continuing to check (since session checks are fast read operations).

---
**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:364**
The same maxSessionCheck pattern appears in `hasAnyLiveSession()` with the same potential issue. When the limit of 50 is reached, the function returns false, which could incorrectly conclude that no live sessions exist if there's a live session beyond the 50th checked. This could cause background auto-apply to run when it shouldn't, potentially causing a race with an active user session.
```suggestion
			wingmanLog("stopping live-session scan after %d entries; assuming live session may exist", checked)
			return true
```

**cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:456**
The `fileExists()` helper function is defined in multiple files (utils.go, strategy/common.go, checkpoint/temporary.go, and used in wingman_review.go at line 485). This duplication violates the DRY principle and creates maintenance burden. Consider consolidating these into a single shared utility location, or using the existing one from utils.go consistently across the codebase.

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"os/exec"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→	"syscall"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.
    16→// repoRoot is the repository root used to locate the log file.
    17→// payloadPath is the path to the JSON payload file.
    18→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    19→// after the parent exits.
    20→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath string) {
    21→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadPath is controlled internally, not user input
    27→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__review", payloadPath)
    28→
    29→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    30→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    31→		Setpgid: true,
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	// Don't hold the working directory
    35→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    36→
    37→	// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)
    38→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    39→
    40→	// Redirect stderr to a log file for debugging the background process.
    41→	// This catches panics, errors, and all wingmanLog() output.
    42→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    43→	var logFile *os.File
    44→	logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
    45→	if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
    46→		//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants
    47→		if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log"),
    48→			os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
    49→			logFile = f
    50→			cmd.Stderr = f
    51→		} else {
    52→			cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    53→		}
    54→	} else {
    55→		cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    59→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    60→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to spawn review subprocess: %v\n", err)
    61→		if logFile != nil {
    62→			_ = logFile.Close()
    63→		}
    64→		return
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	pid := cmd.Process.Pid
    68→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review subprocess spawned (pid=%d)\n", pid)
    69→
    70→	// The log file handle is inherited by the child process and will be
    71→	// closed when that process exits. Don't close it here.
    72→
    73→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    74→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    75→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    76→}
    77→
    78→// spawnDetachedWingmanApply spawns a detached subprocess to auto-apply the
    79→// pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue. Called from the stop hook when a
    80→// review is pending after the agent turn ends.
    81→func spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string) {
    82→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	//nolint:gosec // G204: repoRoot is from paths.RepoRoot(), not user input
    88→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__apply", repoRoot)
    89→
    90→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    91→		Setpgid: true,
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    95→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    96→
    97→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    98→	var applyLogFile *os.File
    99→	logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
   100→	if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
   101→		//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants
   102→		if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log"),
   103→			os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
   104→			applyLogFile = f
   105→			cmd.Stderr = f
   106→		} else {
   107→			cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
   108→		}
   109→	} else {
   110→		cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
   114→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to spawn apply subprocess: %v\n", err)
   115→		if applyLogFile != nil {
   116→			_ = applyLogFile.Close()
   117→		}
   118→		return
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	pid := cmd.Process.Pid
   122→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Apply subprocess spawned (pid=%d)\n", pid)
   123→
   124→	// The log file handle is inherited by the child process and will be
   125→	// closed when that process exits. Don't close it here.
   126→
   127→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
   128→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
   129→}
   130→

    19→// wingmanLog writes a timestamped log line to stderr. In the detached subprocess,
    20→// stderr is redirected to .entire/logs/wingman.log by the spawner.
    21→func wingmanLog(format string, args ...any) {
    22→	msg := fmt.Sprintf(format, args...)
    23→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "%s [wingman] %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), msg)
    24→}
    25→
    26→const (
    27→	// wingmanInitialDelay is how long to wait before starting the review,
    28→	// letting the agent turn fully settle.
    29→	wingmanInitialDelay = 10 * time.Second
    30→
    31→	// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.
    32→	wingmanReviewModel = "sonnet"
    33→
    34→	// wingmanGitTimeout is the timeout for git diff operations.
    35→	wingmanGitTimeout = 60 * time.Second
    36→
    37→	// wingmanReviewTimeout is the timeout for the claude --print review call.
    38→	wingmanReviewTimeout = 5 * time.Minute
    39→
    40→	// wingmanApplyTimeout is the timeout for the claude --continue auto-apply call.
    41→	wingmanApplyTimeout = 15 * time.Minute
    42→)
    43→
    44→// wingmanCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.
    45→type wingmanCLIResponse struct {
    46→	Result string `json:"result"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    50→	return &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:    "__review",
    52→		Hidden: true,
    53→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    54→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    55→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])
    56→		},
    57→	}
    58→}
    59→
    60→func runWingmanReview(payloadPath string) error {
    61→	wingmanLog("review process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
    62→	wingmanLog("reading payload from %s", payloadPath)
    63→
    64→	// Read payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)
    65→	payloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		wingmanLog("ERROR reading payload: %v", err)
    68→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read payload file: %w", err)
    69→	}
    70→	// Clean up payload file after reading
    71→	_ = os.Remove(payloadPath)
    72→
    73→	var payload WingmanPayload
    74→	if err := json.Unmarshal(payloadJSON, &payload); err != nil {
    75→		wingmanLog("ERROR unmarshaling payload: %v", err)
    76→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    80→	if repoRoot == "" {
    81→		wingmanLog("ERROR repo_root is empty in payload")
    82→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
    86→	wingmanLog("session=%s repo=%s base_commit=%s files=%d",
    87→		payload.SessionID, repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit, totalFiles)
    88→
    89→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    90→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    91→	defer func() {
    92→		if err := os.Remove(lockPath); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
    93→			wingmanLog("WARNING: failed to remove lock file: %v", err)
    94→		} else {
    95→			wingmanLog("lock file removed")
    96→		}
    97→	}()
    98→
    99→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
   100→	wingmanLog("waiting %s for agent turn to settle", wingmanInitialDelay)
   101→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
   102→
   103→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
   104→	wingmanLog("computing diff against %s", payload.BaseCommit)
   105→	diffStart := time.Now()
   106→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		wingmanLog("ERROR computing diff: %v", err)
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   113→		wingmanLog("no changes found in diff, exiting")
   114→		return nil // No changes to review
   115→	}
   116→	wingmanLog("diff computed: %d bytes in %s", len(diff), time.Since(diffStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   117→
   118→	// Build file list for the prompt
   119→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   120→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
   121→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
   122→	}
   123→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
   124→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
   125→	}
   126→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
   127→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
   128→	}
   129→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
   130→
   131→	// Read session context from checkpoint data (best-effort)
   132→	sessionContext := readSessionContext(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)
   133→	if sessionContext != "" {
   134→		wingmanLog("session context loaded: %d bytes", len(sessionContext))
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Build review prompt
   138→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, payload.CommitMessage, sessionContext, payload.SessionID, fileList, diff)
   139→	wingmanLog("review prompt built: %d bytes", len(prompt))
   140→
   141→	// Call Claude CLI for review
   142→	wingmanLog("calling claude CLI (model=%s, timeout=%s)", wingmanReviewModel, wingmanReviewTimeout)
   143→	reviewStart := time.Now()
   144→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		wingmanLog("ERROR claude CLI failed after %s: %v", time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   147→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→	wingmanLog("review received: %d bytes in %s", len(reviewText), time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   150→
   151→	// Write REVIEW.md
   152→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   153→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   154→		wingmanLog("ERROR creating directory: %v", err)
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   158→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   159→		wingmanLog("ERROR writing REVIEW.md: %v", err)
   160→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→	wingmanLog("REVIEW.md written to %s", reviewPath)
   163→
   164→	// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using
   165→	// os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   166→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   167→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   168→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   169→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   170→
   171→	saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{
   172→		SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   173→		FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   174→		ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   175→		ReviewApplied: false,
   176→	})
   177→	wingmanLog("dedup state saved")
   178→
   179→	// If any session is live (IDLE/ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED), don't auto-apply
   180→	// in the background. The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as
   181→	// additionalContext when the user sends their next prompt — this is VISIBLE
   182→	// in their terminal. Only use background auto-apply when no sessions are
   183→	// alive (e.g., user closed all sessions).
   184→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   185→		wingmanLog("live session detected, deferring to prompt-submit injection (visible)")
   186→		return nil
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// No live sessions — apply in background as fallback
   190→	wingmanLog("no live sessions, triggering background auto-apply")
   191→	now := time.Now()
   192→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   193→	if state != nil {
   194→		state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
   195→		saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	applyStart := time.Now()
   199→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   200→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   204→
   205→	return nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// computeDiff gets the full branch diff for review. It diffs the current HEAD
   209→// against the merge base with the default branch (main/master), giving the
   210→// reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than just one commit.
   211→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, _ string) (string, error) {
   212→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   213→	defer cancel()
   214→
   215→	// Find the merge base with the default branch for a holistic branch diff.
   216→	mergeBase := findMergeBase(ctx, repoRoot)
   217→	if mergeBase != "" {
   218→		wingmanLog("using merge-base %s for branch diff", mergeBase)
   219→		diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, mergeBase)
   220→		if err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(diff) != "" {
   221→			return diff, nil
   222→		}
   223→		// Fall through to HEAD diff if merge-base diff fails or is empty
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	// Fallback: diff uncommitted changes against HEAD
   227→	wingmanLog("falling back to HEAD diff")
   228→	diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD")
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// If no uncommitted changes, try the latest commit itself
   234→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   235→		diff, err = gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   236→		if err != nil {
   237→			return "", nil
   238→		}
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	return diff, nil
   242→}
   243→
   244→// findMergeBase returns the merge-base commit between HEAD and the default
   245→// branch (tries main, then master). Returns empty string if not found.
   246→func findMergeBase(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string) string {
   247→	for _, branch := range []string{"main", "master"} {
   248→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "merge-base", branch, "HEAD") //nolint:gosec // branch is from hardcoded slice
   249→		cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   250→		out, err := cmd.Output()
   251→		if err == nil {
   252→			return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   253→		}
   254→	}
   255→	return ""
   256→}
   257→
   258→// gitDiff runs git diff with the given args and returns stdout.
   259→func gitDiff(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   260→	fullArgs := append([]string{"diff"}, args...)
   261→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", fullArgs...) //nolint:gosec // args are from internal logic
   262→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   263→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   264→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   265→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   266→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   267→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff %v: %w (stderr: %s)", args, err, stderr.String())
   268→	}
   269→	return stdout.String(), nil
   270→}
   271→
   272→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   273→// repoRoot is the repository root so the reviewer can access the full codebase.
   274→func callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt string) (string, error) {
   275→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanReviewTimeout)
   276→	defer cancel()
   277→
   278→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   279→		"--print",
   280→		"--output-format", "json",
   281→		"--model", wingmanReviewModel,
   282→		"--setting-sources", "",
   283→		// Grant read-only tool access so the reviewer can inspect source files
   284→		// beyond just the diff. Permission bypass is safe here since tools are
   285→		// restricted to read-only operations.
   286→		"--allowedTools", "Read,Glob,Grep",
   287→		"--permission-mode", "bypassPermissions",
   288→	)
   289→
   290→	// Run from repo root so the reviewer can read source files for context.
   291→	// Loop-breaking is handled by --setting-sources "" (disables hooks) and
   292→	// wingmanStripGitEnv (prevents git index pollution).
   293→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   294→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   295→
   296→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   297→
   298→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   299→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   300→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   301→
   302→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   303→		var execErr *exec.Error
   304→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   305→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   306→		}
   307→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   308→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   309→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   310→		}
   311→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Parse the CLI response
   315→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   316→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   317→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   321→}
   322→
   323→// readSessionContext reads the context.md file from the session's checkpoint
   324→// metadata directory. Returns empty string if unavailable.
   325→func readSessionContext(repoRoot, sessionID string) string {
   326→	if sessionID == "" {
   327→		return ""
   328→	}
   329→	contextPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "context.md")
   330→	data, err := os.ReadFile(contextPath) //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repoRoot + session ID
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		return ""
   333→	}
   334→	return string(data)
   335→}
   336→
   337→// staleActiveSessionThreshold is the maximum age of a session state file in an
   338→// ACTIVE phase before it is considered stale (crashed agent) and ignored by
   339→// hasAnyLiveSession. Only applies to ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED phases — an IDLE
   340→// session is always considered live regardless of age (user may just be away).
   341→const staleActiveSessionThreshold = 4 * time.Hour
   342→
   343→// hasAnyLiveSession checks if any session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE,
   344→// ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). Used to decide whether to defer review
   345→// application to the prompt-submit injection (visible to user) vs background
   346→// auto-apply (invisible).
   347→//
   348→// ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions older than staleActiveSessionThreshold are
   349→// skipped as likely orphaned from crashed processes. IDLE sessions are always
   350→// considered live regardless of age.
   351→func hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot string) bool {
   352→	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   353→	if sessDir == "" {
   354→		return false
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
   358→	if err != nil {
   359→		return false
   360→	}
   361→
   362→	const maxCheck = 50
   363→	checked := 0
   364→	for _, entry := range entries {
   365→		if checked >= maxCheck {
   366→			return false
   367→		}
   368→		name := entry.Name()
   369→		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   370→			continue
   371→		}
   372→		checked++
   373→
   374→		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
   375→		phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid)
   376→		if phase == "" || phase == string(session.PhaseEnded) {
   377→			continue
   378→		}
   379→
   380→		// ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions that haven't been updated in a
   381→		// long time are likely orphaned from crashed agents — skip them.
   382→		// IDLE sessions are always considered live (user may just be away).
   383→		if session.Phase(phase).IsActive() {
   384→			info, statErr := entry.Info()
   385→			if statErr != nil {
   386→				continue
   387→			}
   388→			if time.Since(info.ModTime()) > staleActiveSessionThreshold {
   389→				wingmanLog("skipping stale active session %s (phase=%s, age=%s)", sid, phase, time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   390→				continue
   391→			}
   392→		}
   393→
   394→		wingmanLog("found live session %s (phase=%s)", sid, phase)
   395→		return true
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	return false
   399→}
   400→
   401→// findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by
   402→// reading .git to handle both normal repos and worktrees.
   403→func findSessionStateDir(repoRoot string) string {
   404→	gitPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".git")
   405→	info, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
   406→	if err != nil {
   407→		return ""
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	var gitDir string
   411→	if info.IsDir() {
   412→		// Normal repo: .git is a directory
   413→		gitDir = gitPath
   414→	} else {
   415→		// Worktree: .git is a file containing "gitdir: <path>"
   416→		data, readErr := os.ReadFile(gitPath) //nolint:gosec // path from repoRoot
   417→		if readErr != nil {
   418→			return ""
   419→		}
   420→		content := strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
   421→		if !strings.HasPrefix(content, "gitdir: ") {
   422→			return ""
   423→		}
   424→		gitDir = strings.TrimPrefix(content, "gitdir: ")
   425→		if !filepath.IsAbs(gitDir) {
   426→			gitDir = filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   427→		}
   428→		// For worktrees, session state is in the common dir
   429→		// .git/worktrees/<name> → ../../ is the common .git dir
   430→		commonDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "..", "..")
   431→		gitDir = filepath.Clean(commonDir)
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	sessDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "entire-sessions")
   435→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(sessDir); statErr != nil {
   436→		return ""
   437→	}
   438→	return sessDir
   439→}
   440→
   441→// readSessionPhase reads just the phase field from a session state JSON file.
   442→func readSessionPhase(sessDir, sessionID string) string {
   443→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(sessDir, sessionID+".json")) //nolint:gosec // sessDir is from git internals
   444→	if err != nil {
   445→		return ""
   446→	}
   447→	var partial struct {
   448→		Phase string `json:"phase"`
   449→	}
   450→	if json.Unmarshal(data, &partial) != nil {
   451→		return ""
   452→	}
   453→	return partial.Phase
   454→}
   455→
   456→// runWingmanApply is the entrypoint for the __apply subcommand, spawned by the
   457→// stop hook when a pending REVIEW.md is detected. It re-checks preconditions
   458→// and triggers claude --continue to apply the review.
   459→func runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error {
   460→	wingmanLog("apply process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
   461→
   462→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   463→	if !fileExists(reviewPath) {
   464→		wingmanLog("no REVIEW.md found at %s, nothing to apply", reviewPath)
   465→		return nil
   466→	}
   467→	wingmanLog("REVIEW.md found at %s", reviewPath)
   468→
   469→	// Retry prevention: check if apply was already attempted for this review
   470→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   471→	switch {
   472→	case state == nil:
   473→		wingmanLog("no wingman state found, proceeding without session check")
   474→	case state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil:
   475→		wingmanLog("apply already attempted at %s, skipping", state.ApplyAttemptedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   476→		return nil
   477→	default:
   478→		wingmanLog("wingman state loaded: session=%s", state.SessionID)
   479→	}
   480→
   481→	// Re-check session hasn't become active (user may have typed during spawn delay).
   482→	// IDLE and ENDED are safe — only ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED should block.
   483→	if state != nil && state.SessionID != "" {
   484→		sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   485→		if sessDir != "" {
   486→			phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, state.SessionID)
   487→			if phase != "" && session.Phase(phase).IsActive() {
   488→				wingmanLog("session is active (phase=%s), aborting (next stop hook will retry)", phase)
   489→				return nil
   490→			}
   491→			wingmanLog("session phase=%s, safe to proceed", phase)
   492→		}
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	// Mark apply as attempted BEFORE triggering
   496→	if state != nil {
   497→		now := time.Now()
   498→		state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
   499→		saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	wingmanLog("triggering auto-apply via claude --continue")
   503→	applyStart := time.Now()
   504→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   505→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   506→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   509→
   510→	return nil
   511→}
   512→
   513→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   514→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   515→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   516→	defer cancel()
   517→
   518→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   519→		"--continue",
   520→		"--print",
   521→		"--setting-sources", "",
   522→		// Auto-accept edits so the agent can modify files and delete REVIEW.md
   523→		// without requiring user consent (this runs in a background process).
   524→		"--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",
   525→		wingmanApplyInstruction,
   526→	)
   527→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   528→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, and set
   529→	// ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 so git hooks (post-commit) know not to
   530→	// trigger another wingman review (prevents infinite recursion).
   531→	env := wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   532→	env = append(env, "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1")
   533→	cmd.Env = env
   534→
   535→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   536→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   537→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   538→
   539→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   540→}
   541→
   542→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   543→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   544→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   545→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   546→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   547→	for _, e := range env {
   548→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   549→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   550→		}
   551→	}
   552→	return filtered
   553→}
   554→
   555→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   556→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   557→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   558→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   559→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   560→		return
   561→	}
   562→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   563→	if err != nil {
   564→		return
   565→	}
   566→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   567→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   568→}

   337→// triggerWingmanFromCommit builds a wingman payload from the HEAD commit and
   338→// triggers a review. Used by the git post-commit hook for manual-commit strategy
   339→// where files are committed by the user (not by SaveChanges).
   340→func triggerWingmanFromCommit() {
   341→	// Prevent infinite recursion: skip if inside wingman auto-apply
   342→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   343→		return
   344→	}
   345→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   346→		return
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   350→	if err != nil {
   351→		return
   352→	}
   353→
   354→	head := resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   355→	if head == "" {
   356→		return
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	// Get changed files from the commit
   360→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
   361→	defer cancel()
   362→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   363→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff-tree", "--no-commit-id", "--name-status", "-r", head)
   364→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   365→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   366→	if err != nil || len(out) == 0 {
   367→		return
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	var modified, newFiles, deleted []string
   371→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), "\n") {
   372→		if len(line) < 3 {
   373→			continue
   374→		}
   375→		status := line[0]
   376→		file := strings.TrimSpace(line[1:])
   377→		switch status {
   378→		case 'M':
   379→			modified = append(modified, file)
   380→		case 'A':
   381→			newFiles = append(newFiles, file)
   382→		case 'D':
   383→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   384→		}
   385→	}
   386→
   387→	if len(modified)+len(newFiles)+len(deleted) == 0 {
   388→		return
   389→	}

   405→		DeletedFiles:  deleted,
   406→		CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   407→	})
   408→}
   409→
   410→// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
   411→// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
   412→const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
   413→
   414→// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
   415→// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
   416→// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
   417→func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
   418→	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   419→	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   420→	if err != nil {
   421→		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
   422→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
   423→			return false
   424→		}
   425→		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
   426→		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
   427→		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
   428→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   429→			return false
   430→		}
   431→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
   432→			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   433→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   434→		// Retry the atomic create
   435→		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   436→		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   437→		if err != nil {
   438→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
   439→			return false
   440→		}
   441→	}
   442→	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
   443→	_ = f.Close()
   444→	return true
   445→}
   446→
   447→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   448→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   449→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
   450→	defer cancel()
   451→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   452→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   453→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   454→	if err != nil {
   455→		return ""
   456→	}
   457→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   458→}
   459→
   460→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   461→// Uses null byte separator (impossible in filenames) to avoid ambiguity.
   462→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   463→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   464→	copy(sorted, files)
   465→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   466→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   467→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   468→}

```
     1→# Wingman: Automated Code Review
     2→
     3→Wingman is an automated code review system that reviews agent-produced code changes after each commit and delivers actionable suggestions back to the agent.
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→When enabled, wingman runs a background review after each commit (or checkpoint), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and ensures the agent addresses them. The system prioritizes **visible delivery** — the user should see the agent reading and applying the review in their terminal.
     8→
     9→## Architecture
    10→
    11→### Components
    12→
    13→| Component | File | Purpose |
    14→|-----------|------|---------|
    15→| Trigger & state | `wingman.go` | Payload, state management, dedup, lock files, stale review cleanup |
    16→| Review process | `wingman_review.go` | Detached subprocess: diff, Claude review call, REVIEW.md creation, session detection |
    17→| Review prompt | `wingman_prompt.go` | Builds the review prompt from diff, prompts, context |
    18→| Instruction | `wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded instruction injected into agent context |
    19→| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached subprocess spawning (Unix) |
    20→| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stubs (non-Unix) |
    21→| Hook integration | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Prompt-submit injection, stop hook trigger, session-end trigger |
    22→
    23→### State Files
    24→
    25→| File | Purpose |
    26→|------|---------|
    27→| `.entire/REVIEW.md` | The review itself, read by the agent |
    28→| `.entire/wingman-state.json` | Dedup state, session ID, apply tracking |
    29→| `.entire/wingman.lock` | Prevents concurrent review spawns |
    30→| `.entire/wingman-payload.json` | Payload passed to detached review process |
    31→| `.entire/logs/wingman.log` | Review process logs (stderr redirect) |
    32→
    33→## Lifecycle
    34→
    35→### Phase 1: Trigger
    36→
    37→A wingman review is triggered after code changes are committed:
    38→
    39→- **Manual-commit strategy**: Git `post-commit` hook calls `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`
    40→- **Auto-commit strategy**: Stop hook calls `triggerWingmanReview()` after `SaveChanges()`
    41→
    42→Before spawning, preconditions are checked:
    43→1. `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` env var not set (prevents recursion from auto-apply)
    44→2. No pending REVIEW.md for the current session (`shouldSkipPendingReview()`)
    45→3. Lock file acquired atomically (`acquireWingmanLock()`)
    46→4. File hash dedup — skip if same files were already reviewed this session
    47→
    48→### Phase 2: Review (Detached Process)
    49→
    50→The review runs in a fully detached subprocess (`entire wingman __review <payload-path>`):
    51→
    52→```
    53→┌─ Detached Process ─────────────────────────────────────┐
    54→│ 1. Read payload from file                              │
    55→│ 2. Wait 10s for agent turn to settle                   │
    56→│ 3. Compute diff (merge-base with main/master)          │
    57→│ 4. Load session context from checkpoint metadata       │
    58→│ 5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + context)      │
    59→│ 6. Call claude --print (sonnet model, read-only tools)  │
    60→│ 7. Write REVIEW.md                                     │
    61→│ 8. Save dedup state                                    │
    62→│ 9. Determine delivery path (see Phase 3)               │
    63→│ 10. Remove lock file                                   │
    64→└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    65→```
    66→
    67→The review process uses `--setting-sources ""` to disable hooks (prevents recursion) and strips `GIT_*` environment variables for isolation.
    68→
    69→### Phase 3: Delivery
    70→
    71→There are two delivery mechanisms. The system chooses based on whether any session is still alive.
    72→
    73→#### Primary: Prompt-Submit Injection (Visible)
    74→
    75→When a live session exists (IDLE, ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase — excluding stale ACTIVE sessions older than 4 hours), the review is delivered through the agent's next prompt:
    76→
    77→```
    78→Review finishes → REVIEW.md written → live session detected → defer to injection
    79→
    80→User sends prompt → UserPromptSubmit hook fires
    81→                  → REVIEW.md exists on disk
    82→                  → Inject as additionalContext (mandatory agent instruction)
    83→                  → Agent reads REVIEW.md, applies suggestions, deletes file
    84→                  → Agent then proceeds with user's actual request
    85→```
    86→
    87→The `additionalContext` hook response field adds the instruction directly to Claude's context, making it a mandatory pre-step rather than an ignorable warning. The embedded instruction (`wingman_instruction.md`) tells the agent to:
    88→
    89→1. Read `.entire/REVIEW.md`
    90→2. Address each suggestion (skip any it disagrees with)
    91→3. Delete `.entire/REVIEW.md` when done
    92→4. Briefly tell the user what changed
    93→
    94→This path is **visible** — the user sees the agent working through the review in their terminal.
    95→
    96→#### Fallback: Background Auto-Apply (Invisible)
    97→
    98→When no live sessions exist (all ENDED or none), REVIEW.md is applied via a background process:
    99→
   100→```
   101→Review finishes → REVIEW.md written → no live sessions → background auto-apply
   102→
   103→entire wingman __apply <repoRoot>
   104→  → Verify REVIEW.md exists
   105→  → Check ApplyAttemptedAt not set (retry prevention)
   106→  → Re-check session phase is not ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED (guard against race)
   107→  → claude --continue --print --permission-mode acceptEdits
   108→```
   109→
   110→This path is **invisible** — it runs silently. It exists as a fallback for when no session will receive the injection (e.g., user closed all sessions during the review window). Both IDLE and ENDED phases are considered safe for auto-apply — only truly active sessions (ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED) block it.
   111→
   112→### Decision Flow
   113→
   114→```
   115→                    REVIEW.md written
   116→                          │
   117→                          ▼
   118→                ┌─────────────────┐
   119→                │ Any live session │
   120→                │   exists?        │
   121→                └────────┬────────┘
   122→                    │          │
   123→                   Yes         No
   124→                    │          │
   125→                    ▼          ▼
   126→            ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────────┐
   127→            │  Defer   │ │  Background  │
   128→            │  to next │ │  auto-apply  │
   129→            │  prompt  │ │  immediately │
   130→            └──────────┘ └──────────────┘
   131→                    │
   132→                    ▼
   133→            User sends prompt
   134→                    │
   135→                    ▼
   136→            additionalContext
   137→            injection fires
   138→                    │
   139→                    ▼
   140→            Agent applies review
   141→            (visible in terminal)
   142→```
   143→
   144→### Trigger Points Summary
   145→
   146→| Trigger | When | What Happens |
   147→|---------|------|-------------|
   148→| **Review process** (`runWingmanReview`) | Review finishes | If no live sessions → background auto-apply. Otherwise defer. |
   149→| **Prompt-submit hook** (`captureInitialState`) | User sends prompt | If REVIEW.md exists → inject as `additionalContext`. |
   150→| **Stop hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`) | Agent turn ends | If REVIEW.md exists + no live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |
   151→| **Session-end hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd`) | User closes session | If REVIEW.md exists + no remaining live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |
   152→
   153→## Timing
   154→
   155→Typical timeline for a review cycle:
   156→
   157→```
   158→T+0s    Commit happens → wingman review triggered
   159→T+0s    Lock file created, payload written
   160→T+10s   Initial settle delay completes
   161→T+10s   Diff computed (~30-50ms)
   162→T+11s   Claude review API call starts
   163→T+30-50s Review received, REVIEW.md written
   164→T+30-50s Delivery path determined
   165→```
   166→
   167→The 10-second initial delay lets the agent turn fully settle before computing the diff, ensuring all file writes are flushed.
   168→
   169→## Review Prompt Construction
   170→
   171→The review prompt is the core of wingman's value. Unlike a naive diff-only review, it leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built.
   172→
   173→### Context Sources
   174→
   175→The prompt is assembled from six sources, each contributing a different layer of understanding:
   176→
   177→| Source | Origin | What It Provides |
   178→|--------|--------|-----------------|
   179→| **Developer prompts** | `prompt.txt` from checkpoint metadata | The original instructions given to the agent — the ground truth of intent |
   180→| **Commit message** | Git commit or auto-commit message | A summary of what was done (may differ from what was asked) |
   181→| **Session context** | `context.md` from checkpoint metadata | Generated summary of key actions, decisions, and session flow |
   182→| **Checkpoint files** | `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/` | Paths provided so the reviewer can read the full transcript, prompts, or context if needed |
   183→| **File list** | Payload from trigger | Which files changed and how (modified/new/deleted) |
   184→| **Branch diff** | `git diff` against merge-base | The actual code changes — computed against `main`/`master` for a holistic branch-level view |
   185→
   186→### Prompt Structure
   187→
   188→```
   189→┌─ System Role ──────────────────────────────────────────┐
   190→│ "You are a senior code reviewer performing an           │
   191→│  intent-aware review."                                  │
   192→├─ Session Context ──────────────────────────────────────┤
   193→│ Developer's Prompts     <prompts>...</prompts>          │
   194→│ Commit Message          (plain text)                    │
   195→│ Session Context         <session-context>...</session-context> │
   196→│ Checkpoint File Paths   (for deeper investigation)      │
   197→├─ Code Changes ─────────────────────────────────────────┤
   198→│ Files changed:          file.go (modified), ...         │
   199→│ Diff                    <diff>...</diff>                │
   200→├─ Review Instructions ──────────────────────────────────┤
   201→│ Intent alignment        (most important)                │
   202→│ Correctness             bugs, logic errors, races       │
   203→│ Security                injection, secrets, path traversal │
   204→│ Robustness              edge cases, leaks, timeouts     │
   205→│ Do NOT flag             style, docs on clear code       │
   206→│ Output format           Markdown with severity levels   │
   207→└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   208→```
   209→
   210→### Intent-Aware Review
   211→
   212→The review instructions prioritize **intent alignment** as the most important category:
   213→
   214→1. Do the changes actually accomplish what the developer asked for?
   215→2. Are there any prompts or requirements that were missed or only partially implemented?
   216→3. Does the implementation match the stated approach in the session context?
   217→
   218→This is only possible because Entire captures the developer's prompts and session context as checkpoint metadata. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.
   219→
   220→### Diff Strategy
   221→
   222→The diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:
   223→
   224→1. `git merge-base main HEAD` → diff against merge-base (branch-level view)
   225→2. `git merge-base master HEAD` → try master if main doesn't exist
   226→3. `git diff HEAD` → uncommitted changes only
   227→4. `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` → latest commit if no uncommitted changes
   228→
   229→### Read-Only Tool Access
   230→
   231→The reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to:
   232→
   233→- Read source files beyond the diff for additional context
   234→- Search for related code patterns or usages
   235→- Inspect the checkpoint metadata files referenced in the prompt
   236→
   237→Tools are restricted to read-only operations — the reviewer cannot modify files.
   238→
   239→### Truncation
   240→
   241→Diffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. Very large diffs tend to produce unfocused reviews.
   242→
   243→### Output Format
   244→
   245→The reviewer outputs structured Markdown with:
   246→- **Summary**: Does the change accomplish its goal? Overall quality assessment.
   247→- **Issues**: Each with severity (`CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `SUGGESTION`), file path with line reference, description, and suggested fix.
   248→
   249→This output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md` and later read by the agent during delivery.
   250→
   251→## Stale Review Cleanup
   252→
   253→Reviews can become stale in several scenarios. The `shouldSkipPendingReview()` function handles cleanup:
   254→
   255→| Scenario | Detection | Action |
   256→|----------|-----------|--------|
   257→| REVIEW.md without state file | `state == nil` | Delete REVIEW.md (orphan) |
   258→| REVIEW.md from different session | `state.SessionID != currentSessionID` | Delete REVIEW.md (stale) |
   259→| REVIEW.md older than 1 hour | `time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1h` | Delete REVIEW.md (TTL expired) |
   260→| REVIEW.md from current session | Session matches + fresh | Keep (skip new review) |
   261→
   262→## Retry Prevention
   263→
   264→The `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply attempts:
   265→
   266→- Set to current time before triggering auto-apply
   267→- Reset to `nil` when a new review is written
   268→- Checked before every auto-apply attempt — if set, skip
   269→
   270→## Concurrency Safety
   271→
   272→- **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.
   273→- **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.
   274→- **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.
   275→- **GIT_* stripping**: Subprocesses strip all `GIT_*` env vars to prevent index corruption.
   276→- **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).
   277→- **Stale session detection**: ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions not updated in 4+ hours are considered orphaned (crashed agent) and ignored by `hasAnyLiveSession`. IDLE sessions are always considered live regardless of age.
   278→
   279→## Configuration
   280→
   281→```bash
   282→entire wingman enable   # Enable wingman auto-review
   283→entire wingman disable  # Disable and clean up pending reviews
   284→entire wingman status   # Show current status
   285→```
   286→
   287→Wingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:
   288→
   289→```json
   290→{
   291→  "strategy_options": {
   292→    "wingman": {
   293→      "enabled": true
   294→    }
   295→  }
   296→}
   297→```
   298→
   299→## Key Constants
   300→
   301→| Constant | Value | Purpose |
   302→|----------|-------|---------|
   303→| `wingmanInitialDelay` | 10s | Settle time before computing diff |
   304→| `wingmanReviewModel` | `sonnet` | Model used for reviews |
   305→| `wingmanGitTimeout` | 60s | Timeout for git diff operations |
   306→| `wingmanReviewTimeout` | 5m | Timeout for Claude review API call |
   307→| `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |
   308→| `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |
   309→| `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |
   310→| `staleActiveSessionThreshold` | 4h | Max age before ACTIVE session is considered stale/orphaned |
   311→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/docs/architecture/wingman.md",
  "content": "# Wingman: Automated Code Review\n\nWingman is an automated code review system that reviews agent-produced code changes after each commit and delivers actionable suggestions back to the agent.\n\n## Overview\n\nWhen enabled, wingman runs a background review after each commit (or checkpoint), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and ensures the agent addresses them. The system prioritizes **visible delivery** — the user should see the agent reading and applying the review in their terminal.\n\n## Architecture\n\n### Components\n\n| Component | File | Purpose |\n|-----------|------|---------|\n| Trigger & state | `wingman.go` | Payload, state management, dedup, lock files, stale review cleanup |\n| Review process | `wingman_review.go` | Detached subprocess: diff, Claude review call, REVIEW.md creation, session detection |\n| Review prompt | `wingman_prompt.go` | Builds the review prompt from diff, prompts, context |\n| Instruction | `wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded instruction injected into agent context |\n| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached subprocess spawning (Unix) |\n| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stubs (non-Unix) |\n| Hook integration | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Prompt-submit injection, stop hook notifications, session-end trigger |\n\n### State Files\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `.entire/REVIEW.md` | The review itself, read by the agent |\n| `.entire/wingman-state.json` | Dedup state, session ID, apply tracking |\n| `.entire/wingman.lock` | Prevents concurrent review spawns |\n| `.entire/wingman-payload.json` | Payload passed to detached review process |\n| `.entire/logs/wingman.log` | Review process logs (stderr redirect) |\n\n## Lifecycle\n\n### Phase 1: Trigger\n\nA wingman review is triggered after code changes are committed:\n\n- **Manual-commit strategy**: Git `post-commit` hook calls `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`\n- **Auto-commit strategy**: Stop hook calls `triggerWingmanReview()` after `SaveChanges()`\n\nBefore spawning, preconditions are checked:\n1. `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` env var not set (prevents recursion from auto-apply)\n2. No pending REVIEW.md for the current session (`shouldSkipPendingReview()`)\n3. Lock file acquired atomically (`acquireWingmanLock()`)\n4. File hash dedup — skip if same files were already reviewed this session\n\n### Phase 2: Review (Detached Process)\n\nThe review runs in a fully detached subprocess (`entire wingman __review <payload-path>`):\n\n```\n┌─ Detached Process ─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ 1. Read payload from file                              │\n│ 2. Wait 10s for agent turn to settle                   │\n│ 3. Compute diff (merge-base with main/master)          │\n│ 4. Load session context from checkpoint metadata       │\n│ 5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + context)      │\n│ 6. Call claude --print (sonnet model, read-only tools)  │\n│ 7. Write REVIEW.md                                     │\n│ 8. Save dedup state                                    │\n│ 9. Determine delivery path (see Phase 3)               │\n│ 10. Remove lock file                                   │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nThe review process uses `--setting-sources \"\"` to disable hooks (prevents recursion). The Claude CLI calls (`callClaudeForReview` and `triggerAutoApply`) strip `GIT_*` environment variables via `wingmanStripGitEnv()` to prevent index corruption — the detached process itself inherits the parent's environment.\n\n### Phase 3: Delivery\n\nThere are two delivery mechanisms. The system chooses based on whether any session is still alive.\n\n#### Primary: Prompt-Submit Injection (Visible)\n\nWhen a live session exists (IDLE, ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase — excluding stale ACTIVE sessions older than 4 hours), the review is delivered through the agent's next prompt:\n\n```\nUser sends prompt → UserPromptSubmit hook fires\n                  → REVIEW.md exists on disk\n                  → Inject as additionalContext (mandatory agent instruction)\n                  → Agent reads REVIEW.md, applies suggestions, deletes file\n                  → Agent then proceeds with user's actual request\n```\n\nThe `additionalContext` hook response field adds the instruction directly to Claude's context, making it a mandatory pre-step. The embedded instruction (`wingman_instruction.md`) tells the agent to read the review, address suggestions, delete the file, and briefly tell the user what changed.\n\n#### Fallback: Background Auto-Apply (Invisible)\n\nWhen no live sessions exist (all ENDED or none), REVIEW.md is applied via a background process:\n\n```\nentire wingman __apply <repoRoot>\n  → Verify REVIEW.md exists\n  → Check ApplyAttemptedAt not set (retry prevention)\n  → Re-check session phase is not ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED (guard against race)\n  → claude --continue --print --permission-mode acceptEdits\n```\n\nThis path is **invisible** — it runs silently. It exists as a fallback for when no session will receive the injection (e.g., user closed all sessions during the review window). Both IDLE and ENDED phases are considered safe for auto-apply — only truly active sessions (ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED) block it.\n\n### Trigger Points\n\n| Trigger | When | What Happens |\n|---------|------|-------------|\n| **Review process** (`runWingmanReview`) | Review finishes | If no live sessions → background auto-apply. Otherwise defer. |\n| **Prompt-submit hook** (`captureInitialState`) | User sends prompt | If REVIEW.md exists → inject as `additionalContext`. |\n| **Stop hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`) | Agent turn ends | If REVIEW.md exists + no live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |\n| **Session-end hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd`) | User closes session | If REVIEW.md exists + no remaining live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |\n\n## User-Visible Messages\n\nWingman outputs `systemMessage` notifications at key points so the user can see what wingman is doing in their agent terminal. These are informational only — they are NOT injected into the agent's context.\n\n| Message | Hook | Condition | Purpose |\n|---------|------|-----------|---------|\n| `[Wingman] A code review is pending and will be addressed before your request.` | Prompt-submit | REVIEW.md exists (with `additionalContext` injection) | Tells user the agent will apply a review first |\n| `[Wingman] Review in progress...` | Prompt-submit | Lock file exists (no REVIEW.md) | Tells user a review is running in the background |\n| `[Wingman] Reviewing your changes...` | Stop | Lock file exists | Tells user a review was triggered and is still running |\n| `[Wingman] Review pending — will be addressed on your next prompt` | Stop | REVIEW.md exists (no lock file) | Tells user a completed review will be delivered next prompt |\n\nThe prompt-submit REVIEW.md injection message is paired with `additionalContext` — the agent sees and acts on the review. All other messages use `outputHookMessage()` which emits `systemMessage`-only JSON (visible in terminal, invisible to agent).\n\n## Timing\n\nTypical timeline for a review cycle:\n\n```\nT+0s    Commit happens → wingman review triggered\nT+0s    Lock file created, payload written\nT+10s   Initial settle delay completes\nT+10s   Diff computed (~30-50ms)\nT+11s   Claude review API call starts\nT+30-50s Review received, REVIEW.md written\nT+30-50s Delivery path determined\n```\n\nThe 10-second initial delay lets the agent turn fully settle before computing the diff, ensuring all file writes are flushed.\n\n## Review Prompt Construction\n\nThe review prompt leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.\n\n### Context Sources\n\n| Source | Origin | What It Provides |\n|--------|--------|-----------------|\n| **Developer prompts** | `prompt.txt` from checkpoint metadata | The original instructions given to the agent — the ground truth of intent |\n| **Commit message** | Git commit or auto-commit message | A summary of what was done (may differ from what was asked) |\n| **Session context** | `context.md` from checkpoint metadata | Generated summary of key actions, decisions, and session flow |\n| **Checkpoint files** | `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/` | Paths provided so the reviewer can read the full transcript, prompts, or context if needed |\n| **File list** | Payload from trigger | Which files changed and how (modified/new/deleted) |\n| **Branch diff** | `git diff` against merge-base | The actual code changes — computed against `main`/`master` for a holistic branch-level view |\n\n### Diff Strategy\n\nThe diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:\n\n1. `git merge-base main HEAD` → diff against merge-base (branch-level view)\n2. `git merge-base master HEAD` → try master if main doesn't exist\n3. `git diff HEAD` → uncommitted changes only\n4. `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` → latest commit if no uncommitted changes\n\n### Read-Only Tool Access\n\nThe reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to read source files beyond the diff, search for related patterns, and inspect checkpoint metadata. Tools are restricted to read-only operations.\n\n### Output Format\n\nThe reviewer outputs structured Markdown with:\n- **Summary**: Does the change accomplish its goal? Overall quality assessment.\n- **Issues**: Each with severity (`CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `SUGGESTION`), file path with line reference, description, and suggested fix.\n\nDiffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. The output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md`.\n\n## Stale Review Cleanup\n\nReviews can become stale in several scenarios. The `shouldSkipPendingReview()` function handles cleanup:\n\n| Scenario | Detection | Action |\n|----------|-----------|--------|\n| REVIEW.md without state file | `state == nil` | Delete REVIEW.md (orphan) |\n| REVIEW.md from different session | `state.SessionID != currentSessionID` | Delete REVIEW.md (stale) |\n| REVIEW.md older than 1 hour | `time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1h` | Delete REVIEW.md (TTL expired) |\n| REVIEW.md from current session | Session matches + fresh | Keep (skip new review) |\n\n## Retry Prevention\n\nThe `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply attempts:\n\n- Set to current time before triggering auto-apply\n- Reset to `nil` when a new review is written\n- Checked before every auto-apply attempt — if set, skip\n\n## Concurrency Safety\n\n- **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.\n- **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.\n- **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.\n- **GIT_* stripping**: Claude CLI calls strip `GIT_*` env vars via `wingmanStripGitEnv()` to prevent index corruption. Applied in `callClaudeForReview()` and `triggerAutoApply()`, not at process spawn time.\n- **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).\n- **Stale session detection**: ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions not updated in 4+ hours are considered orphaned (crashed agent) and ignored by `hasAnyLiveSession`. IDLE sessions are always considered live regardless of age.\n\n## Configuration\n\n```bash\nentire wingman enable   # Enable wingman auto-review\nentire wingman disable  # Disable and clean up pending reviews\nentire wingman status   # Show current status\n```\n\nWingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"strategy_options\": {\n    \"wingman\": {\n      \"enabled\": true\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Key Constants\n\n| Constant | Value | Purpose |\n|----------|-------|---------|\n| `wingmanInitialDelay` | 10s | Settle time before computing diff |\n| `wingmanReviewModel` | `sonnet` | Model used for reviews |\n| `wingmanGitTimeout` | 60s | Timeout for git diff operations |\n| `wingmanReviewTimeout` | 5m | Timeout for Claude review API call |\n| `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |\n| `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |\n| `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |\n| `staleActiveSessionThreshold` | 4h | Max age before ACTIVE session is considered stale/orphaned |\n"
}

   154→### Diff Strategy
   155→
   156→The diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:
   157→
   158→1. `git merge-base main HEAD` → diff against merge-base (branch-level view)
   159→2. `git merge-base master HEAD` → try master if main doesn't exist
   160→3. `git diff HEAD` → uncommitted changes only
   161→4. `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` → latest commit if no uncommitted changes
   162→
   163→### Read-Only Tool Access
   164→
   165→The reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to read source files beyond the diff, search for related patterns, and inspect checkpoint metadata. Tools are restricted to read-only operations.
   166→
   167→### Output Format
   168→
   169→The reviewer outputs structured Markdown with:
   170→- **Summary**: Does the change accomplish its goal? Overall quality assessment.
   171→- **Issues**: Each with severity (`CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `SUGGESTION`), file path with line reference, description, and suggested fix.
   172→
   173→Diffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. The output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md`.
   174→
   175→## Stale Review Cleanup
   176→
   177→Reviews can become stale in several scenarios. The `shouldSkipPendingReview()` function handles cleanup:
   178→
   179→| Scenario | Detection | Action |
   180→|----------|-----------|--------|
   181→| REVIEW.md without state file | `state == nil` | Delete REVIEW.md (orphan) |
   182→| REVIEW.md from different session | `state.SessionID != currentSessionID` | Delete REVIEW.md (stale) |
   183→| REVIEW.md older than 1 hour | `time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1h` | Delete REVIEW.md (TTL expired) |
   184→| REVIEW.md from current session | Session matches + fresh | Keep (skip new review) |
   185→
   186→## Retry Prevention
   187→
   188→The `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply attempts:
   189→
   190→- Set to current time before triggering auto-apply
   191→- Reset to `nil` when a new review is written
   192→- Checked before every auto-apply attempt — if set, skip
   193→
   194→## Concurrency Safety
   195→
   196→- **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.
   197→- **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.
   198→- **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.
   199→- **GIT_* stripping**: Claude CLI calls strip `GIT_*` env vars via `wingmanStripGitEnv()` to prevent index corruption. Applied in `callClaudeForReview()` and `triggerAutoApply()`, not at process spawn time.
   200→- **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).
   201→- **Stale session detection**: ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions not updated in 4+ hours are considered orphaned (crashed agent) and ignored by `hasAnyLiveSession`. IDLE sessions are always considered live regardless of age.
   202→
   203→## Configuration
   204→
   205→```bash
   206→entire wingman enable   # Enable wingman auto-review
   207→entire wingman disable  # Disable and clean up pending reviews
   208→entire wingman status   # Show current status
   209→```
   210→
   211→Wingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:
   212→
   213→```json
   214→{
   215→  "strategy_options": {
   216→    "wingman": {
   217→      "enabled": true
   218→    }
   219→  }
   220→}
   221→```
   222→
   223→## Key Constants
   224→
   225→| Constant | Value | Purpose |
   226→|----------|-------|---------|
   227→| `wingmanInitialDelay` | 10s | Settle time before computing diff |
   228→| `wingmanReviewModel` | `sonnet` | Model used for reviews |
   229→| `wingmanGitTimeout` | 60s | Timeout for git diff operations |
   230→| `wingmanReviewTimeout` | 5m | Timeout for Claude review API call |
   231→| `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |
   232→| `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |
   233→| `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |
   234→| `staleActiveSessionThreshold` | 4h | Max age before ACTIVE session is considered stale/orphaned |
   235→
```

+## User-Visible Messages
+
+Wingman outputs `systemMessage` notifications at key points so the user can see what wingman is doing in their agent terminal. These are informational only — they are NOT injected into the agent's context.
+
+| Message | Hook | Condition | Purpose |
+|---------|------|-----------|---------|
+| `[Wingman] A code review is pending and will be addressed before your request.` | Prompt-submit | REVIEW.md exists (with `additionalContext` injection) | Tells user the agent will apply a review first |
+| `[Wingman] Review in progress...` | Prompt-submit | Lock file exists (no REVIEW.md) | Tells user a review is running in the background |
+| `[Wingman] Reviewing your changes...` | Stop | Lock file exists | Tells user a review was triggered and is still running |
+| `[Wingman] Review pending — will be addressed on your next prompt` | Stop | REVIEW.md exists (no lock file) | Tells user a completed review will be delivered next prompt |
+
+The prompt-submit REVIEW.md injection message is paired with `additionalContext` — the agent sees and acts on the review. All other messages use `outputHookMessage()` which emits `systemMessage`-only JSON (visible in terminal, invisible to agent).
+
