 ## Review Prompt Construction
 
-The review prompt is the core of wingman's value. Unlike a naive diff-only review, it leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built.
+The review prompt leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.
 
 ### Context Sources
 
-The prompt is assembled from six sources, each contributing a different layer of understanding:
-
 | Source | Origin | What It Provides |
 |--------|--------|-----------------|
 | **Developer prompts** | `prompt.txt` from checkpoint metadata | The original instructions given to the agent — the ground truth of intent |
@@ -183,40 +151,6 @@ The prompt is assembled from six sources, each contributing a different layer of
 | **File list** | Payload from trigger | Which files changed and how (modified/new/deleted) |
 | **Branch diff** | `git diff` against merge-base | The actual code changes — computed against `main`/`master` for a holistic branch-level view |
 
-### Prompt Structure
-
-```
-┌─ System Role ──────────────────────────────────────────┐
-│ "You are a senior code reviewer performing an           │
-│  intent-aware review."                                  │
-├─ Session Context ──────────────────────────────────────┤
-│ Developer's Prompts     <prompts>...</prompts>          │
-│ Commit Message          (plain text)                    │
-│ Session Context         <session-context>...</session-context> │
-│ Checkpoint File Paths   (for deeper investigation)      │
-├─ Code Changes ─────────────────────────────────────────┤
-│ Files changed:          file.go (modified), ...         │
-│ Diff                    <diff>...</diff>                │
-├─ Review Instructions ──────────────────────────────────┤
-│ Intent alignment        (most important)                │
-│ Correctness             bugs, logic errors, races       │
-│ Security                injection, secrets, path traversal │
-│ Robustness              edge cases, leaks, timeouts     │
-│ Do NOT flag             style, docs on clear code       │
-│ Output format           Markdown with severity levels   │
-└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
-```
-
-### Intent-Aware Review
-
-The review instructions prioritize **intent alignment** as the most important category:
-
-1. Do the changes actually accomplish what the developer asked for?
-2. Are there any prompts or requirements that were missed or only partially implemented?
-3. Does the implementation match the stated approach in the session context?
-
-This is only possible because Entire captures the developer's prompts and session context as checkpoint metadata. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.
-
 ### Diff Strategy
 
 The diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:
@@ -228,17 +162,7 @@ The diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just t
 
 ### Read-Only Tool Access
 
-The reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to:
-
-- Read source files beyond the diff for additional context
-- Search for related code patterns or usages
-- Inspect the checkpoint metadata files referenced in the prompt
-
-Tools are restricted to read-only operations — the reviewer cannot modify files.
-
-### Truncation
-
-Diffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. Very large diffs tend to produce unfocused reviews.
+The reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to read source files beyond the diff, search for related patterns, and inspect checkpoint metadata. Tools are restricted to read-only operations.
 
 ### Output Format
 
@@ -246,7 +170,7 @@ The reviewer outputs structured Markdown with:
 - **Summary**: Does the change accomplish its goal? Overall quality assessment.
 - **Issues**: Each with severity (`CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `SUGGESTION`), file path with line reference, description, and suggested fix.
 
-This output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md` and later read by the agent during delivery.
+Diffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. The output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md`.
 
 ## Stale Review Cleanup
 
@@ -272,8 +196,9 @@ The `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply atte
 - **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.
 - **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.
 - **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.
-- **GIT_* stripping**: Subprocesses strip all `GIT_*` env vars to prevent index corruption.
+- **GIT_* stripping**: Claude CLI calls strip `GIT_*` env vars via `wingmanStripGitEnv()` to prevent index corruption. Applied in `callClaudeForReview()` and `triggerAutoApply()`, not at process spawn time.
 - **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).
+- **Stale session detection**: ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions not updated in 4+ hours are considered orphaned (crashed agent) and ignored by `hasAnyLiveSession`. IDLE sessions are always considered live regardless of age.
 
 ## Configuration
 
@@ -306,3 +231,4 @@ Wingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:
 | `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |
 | `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |
 | `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |
+| `staleActiveSessionThreshold` | 4h | Max age before ACTIVE session is considered stale/orphaned |
```

Pushed as `118936db`. Here's what was done:

**PR review fixes (3 code changes):**
- `hasAnyLiveSession` now returns `true` when the 50-entry scan limit is hit — safer to assume a live session exists than to incorrectly spawn background auto-apply
- `computeDiff` fallback now returns the error when `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` fails instead of silently returning `("", nil)`
- Removed unused `baseCommit` parameter from `computeDiff`

**Documentation updates:**
- Added **User-Visible Messages** table documenting all 4 `systemMessage` notifications, which hook they fire from, and their conditions
- Fixed inaccurate claim that the detached process strips `GIT_*` vars (it's actually the Claude CLI calls that do this via `wingmanStripGitEnv()`)
- Removed redundant Decision Flow ASCII diagram (the Trigger Points table is clearer)
- Removed redundant Prompt Structure box and Intent-Aware Review section (consolidated into the Review Prompt Construction intro)

   139→## Review Prompt Construction
   140→
   141→The review prompt leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.
   142→
   143→### Context Sources
   144→
   145→| Source | Origin | What It Provides |
   146→|--------|--------|-----------------|
   147→| **Developer prompts** | `prompt.txt` from checkpoint metadata | The original instructions given to the agent — the ground truth of intent |
   148→| **Commit message** | Git commit or auto-commit message | A summary of what was done (may differ from what was asked) |
   149→| **Session context** | `context.md` from checkpoint metadata | Generated summary of key actions, decisions, and session flow |
   150→| **Checkpoint files** | `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/` | Paths provided so the reviewer can read the full transcript, prompts, or context if needed |
   151→| **File list** | Payload from trigger | Which files changed and how (modified/new/deleted) |
   152→| **Branch diff** | `git diff` against merge-base | The actual code changes — computed against `main`/`master` for a holistic branch-level view |
   153→
   154→### Diff Strategy
   155→
   156→The diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:
   157→
   158→1. `git merge-base main HEAD` → diff against merge-base (branch-level view)
```

> AGENT

Found it. The lock file contains `test` and was created at 16:17 — after the last real review finished at 16:15. It's a leftover from manual testing, but the real problem is clear: `outputWingmanStopNotification` doesn't check if the lock is stale. It treats any lock file as "review running" even if it's hours old from a crashed process.

The fix: check the lock file age against `staleLockThreshold` before showing the notification.

> AGENT

The problem: `outputWingmanStopNotification` and the prompt-submit lock check used `os.Stat` to check if the lock file *exists*, but never checked its age. A stale lock (from a crashed review process or manual testing — yours contained `test`) would trigger "Reviewing your changes..." on every single stop hook.

The fix: both lock file checks now use `time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold` (30 min), matching the same staleness logic already used in `acquireWingmanLock`.

> AGENT

The lock file is from 16:17, contains `test` (manually created), and the last real review finished at 16:15. No reviews since. The problem: `staleLockThreshold` is 30 minutes — way too generous for a notification. A real review takes ~30-60 seconds (max ~5 min with API timeout). The lock is still "fresh" by the 30-minute standard.

The fix: use a tighter threshold for notifications. A review can take at most `wingmanInitialDelay` (10s) + `wingmanReviewTimeout` (5min) = ~5min. Anything older than that is stale for notification purposes.

> AGENT

The root cause: `staleLockThreshold` (30 min) was designed for lock *acquisition* — deciding when to forcibly overwrite a stale lock. But notifications were reusing it, so a lock file up to 30 minutes old would trigger "Reviewing your changes..." even though a real review completes in ~30-60 seconds (max ~6 min with API timeout).

The fix: introduced `wingmanNotificationLockThreshold` (10 min) — used only for notifications in both the stop hook and prompt-submit hook. The lock acquisition logic still uses the original 30-minute threshold.

   772→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   773→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   774→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   775→}
   776→
   777→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   778→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   779→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   780→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   781→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   782→	if err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→
   786→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   787→	if err != nil {
   788→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   789→	}
   790→
   791→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   792→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   793→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   794→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   795→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   796→	)
   797→
   798→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   799→		return nil // No session to update
   800→	}
   801→
   802→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   803→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   804→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   805→	}
   806→
   807→	// If a wingman review is pending and this was the last live session,
   808→	// trigger background auto-apply. No more prompts will come from this
   809→	// session, so the prompt-submit injection can't deliver the review.

   878→// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).
   879→//
   880→// When a live session exists, this is a no-op: the prompt-submit injection
   881→// will deliver the review visibly in the user's terminal instead. Background
   882→// auto-apply is only used when no sessions are alive (all ended).
   883→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
   884→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   885→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   886→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply skip: wingman not enabled")
   887→		return
   888→	}
   889→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   890→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply skip: already in apply subprocess")
   891→		return
   892→	}
   893→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   894→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
   895→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply skip: no REVIEW.md pending")
   896→		return
   897→	}
   898→	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   899→	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   900→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping",
   901→			slog.Time("attempted_at", *wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt),
   902→		)
   903→		return
   904→	}
   905→	// Don't spawn background auto-apply if a live session exists.
   906→	// The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as additionalContext,
   907→	// which is visible to the user in their terminal.
   908→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   909→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred: live session will handle via injection")
   910→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review pending — will be injected on next prompt\n")
   911→		return
   912→	}
   913→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply (no live sessions)\n")
   914→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning (no live sessions)",
   915→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
   916→	)
   917→	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
   918→}

   921→// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.

   921→// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.
   922→// Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd hooks.
   923→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd() {
   924→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   925→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   926→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end skip: wingman not enabled")

   931→		return

   941→		return
   942→	}

   946→			slog.Time("attempted_at", *wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt),
   947→		)
   948→		return
   949→	}
   950→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   951→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred on session-end: other live sessions remain")
   952→		return
   953→	}
   954→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Last session ended with pending review, spawning auto-apply\n")
   955→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end (no live sessions remain)",
   956→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),

> AGENT

Found it. The timeline tells the full story:

```
16:51:53  session-end → SessionStop: idle → ended  ✓  (session correctly marked ENDED)
16:53:34  session-start (THIS session) → SessionStart: ended → idle  ← REVIVED!
```

The `handleSessionStartCommon` fires a `SessionStart` event which transitions `ENDED → IDLE`. The SessionStart hook for the **new** Claude Code conversation runs with `session_id: 266b3f40` (the same Entire session ID gets reused), and it revives the session back to `idle`.

So the sequence was:
1. User closed old session → correctly marked ENDED
2. `triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd` ran and called `hasAnyLiveSession` — but `test.json` (active_committed, 4min old) blocked auto-apply
3. User opened new session → revived `266b3f40` back to IDLE

Two independent blockers:
1. **`test.json`** stale session state with `active_committed` blocked auto-apply even after `266b3f40` was correctly ended
2. **Session revival** — the new session immediately revived the old session state before auto-apply could run

Now let me fix the SessionStart message and clean up the stale test session. The auto-apply blocking by `test.json` is the actionable bug.

Then 2 seconds later, the new session revived the state back to IDLE.

The `test.json` is the real blocker — it's a testing artifact that shouldn't be there. The code logic is correct; it was just blocked by a stale session state. No code fix needed for the auto-apply flow itself.

   241→// handleSessionStartCommon is the shared implementation for session start hooks.
   242→// Used by both Claude Code and Gemini CLI handlers.
   243→func handleSessionStartCommon() error {
   244→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   255→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
   256→		slog.String("hook", "session-start"),
   257→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   258→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   259→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   260→	)
   261→
   262→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   263→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Build informational message
   267→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
   268→
   269→	// Append wingman note if enabled
   270→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   271→		message += "\n  Wingman is active: your changes will be automatically reviewed."
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
   275→	strat := GetStrategy()
   276→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
   277→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(input.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
   278→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
   279→		}
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Output informational message using agent-specific format
   283→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
   284→		return err
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
   288→	// This handles ENDED → IDLE (re-entering a session).
   289→	// TODO(ENT-221): dispatch ActionWarnStaleSession for ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions.
   290→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(input.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
   291→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
   292→	} else if state != nil {
   293→		strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{})
   294→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   295→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	return nil
   300→}

     1→# Code Review
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→This change successfully implements wingman auto-review functionality with pending review indication in SessionStart messages. The implementation is comprehensive, with proper deduplication, lock handling, and integration into both Claude Code and Gemini CLI hooks. The code quality is high with extensive test coverage. However, there are a few issues that need attention.
     5→
     6→## Issues
     7→
     8→### [CRITICAL] Missing file existence check before opening
     9→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go:46`
    10→**Description:** The code opens a log file for append without checking if the parent directory exists. While `MkdirAll` is called for `logDir`, if it fails the code proceeds to open the file anyway, which will fail.
    11→**Suggestion:**
    12→```go
    13→logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
    14→if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
    15→    logPath := filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log")
    16→    if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(logPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
    17→        logFile = f
    18→        cmd.Stderr = f
    19→    } else {
    20→        cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    21→    }
    22→} else {
    23→    cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    24→}
    25→```
    26→
    27→### [WARNING] Race condition in hasAnyLiveSession check
    28→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:185-194`
    29→**Description:** In `runWingmanReview`, after checking `hasAnyLiveSession()` to decide whether to auto-apply, there's a window where a new session could start before the background auto-apply completes. The apply process rechecks this, but the initial decision to set `ApplyAttemptedAt` happens before the recheck.
    30→**Suggestion:**
    31→Move the `ApplyAttemptedAt` setting to inside `runWingmanApply` where the recheck happens:
    32→```go
    33→// In runWingmanReview, remove the ApplyAttemptedAt setting:
    34→if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
    35→    wingmanLog("live session detected, deferring to prompt-submit injection (visible)")
    36→    return nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→wingmanLog("no live sessions, triggering background auto-apply")
    40→applyStart := time.Now()
    41→if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
    42→    // ... error handling
    43→}
    44→
    45→// In runWingmanApply, set ApplyAttemptedAt after the session phase recheck:
    46→if state != nil && session.Phase(phase).IsActive() {
    47→    wingmanLog("session is active (phase=%s), aborting", phase)
    48→    return nil
    49→}
    50→
    51→// Mark as attempted here, after confirming safe to proceed
    52→if state != nil {
    53→    now := time.Now()
    54→    state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
    55→    saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
    56→}
    57→```
    58→
    59→### [WARNING] Unbounded ReadDir in hasAnyLiveSession
    60→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:356-360`
    61→**Description:** `os.ReadDir(sessDir)` loads all directory entries into memory. For repositories with many historical sessions, this could be expensive. The code has a `maxCheck` limit but still reads the entire directory first.
    62→**Suggestion:**
    63→Consider using a more efficient approach or documenting the assumption that session directories won't grow unboundedly. Alternatively, break early after finding a live session without reading the full directory.
    64→
    65→### [WARNING] Session context read without error handling context
    66→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:132`
    67→**Description:** `readSessionContext` silently returns empty string on any error. If the context file is corrupted (partial write, invalid UTF-8), the review proceeds without context but doesn't log why.
    68→**Suggestion:**
    69→```go
    70→func readSessionContext(repoRoot, sessionID string) string {
    71→    if sessionID == "" {
    72→        return ""
    73→    }
    74→    contextPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "context.md")
    75→    data, err := os.ReadFile(contextPath)
    76→    if err != nil {
    77→        if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
    78→            wingmanLog("WARNING: failed to read session context: %v", err)
    79→        }
    80→        return ""
    81→    }
    82→    return string(data)
    83→}
    84→```
    85→
    86→### [SUGGESTION] Unclear mutex-free concurrent access pattern
    87→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go:419-433`
    88→**Description:** `acquireWingmanLock` uses atomic file creation (O_EXCL) for the lock, which is correct. However, the stale lock cleanup (check mtime, remove, retry) has a small TOCTOU window where two processes could both see a stale lock, both remove it, and both succeed in creating new locks. This is unlikely but possible.
    89→**Suggestion:**
    90→Document this race as acceptable (since reviews are idempotent and dedup will catch it), or use a more robust approach like writing PID to the lock file and checking if the process is still alive before removing.
    91→
    92→### [SUGGESTION] Magic string in wingman notification
    93→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:842`
    94→**Description:** The Unicode em-dash character `\u2014` is hardcoded in a user-facing string. While this works, it's less maintainable than using the literal character or a named constant.
    95→**Suggestion:**
    96→```go
    97→_ = outputHookMessage("[Wingman] Review pending — will be addressed on your next prompt")
    98→```
    99→
   100→### [SUGGESTION] Inconsistent error wrapping
   101→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:495`
   102→**Description:** `triggerAutoApply` returns unwrapped errors from `cmd.Run()` via `//nolint:wrapcheck`, but callers wrap them. This is intentional per the comment, but makes error context less useful.
   103→**Suggestion:**
   104→Consider wrapping with additional context about the command that failed:
   105→```go
   106→if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   107→    return fmt.Errorf("claude --continue failed: %w", err)
   108→}
   109→```
```

> AGENT

So the flow is: `runWingmanReview` finishes the review, writes `REVIEW.md`, then checks `hasAnyLiveSession`. If a session is live, it defers to prompt-submit. The problem is: the review runs as a background process after the Stop hook, but by that time the session might already appear as ENDED (since Stop hook transitions it). But there's also the timing — the review takes time (calling Claude), and during that time a NEW session might start.

Let me look at what happens when the user closes a session. The Stop hook fires, transitions the session to ENDED. Then the wingman review runs in background. But the review takes time. After the review completes, `hasAnyLiveSession` checks session states.

The key question is: when the user closes the session and NO new session opens, does `hasAnyLiveSession` still return true?

   337→	return string(data)
   338→}
   339→
   340→// staleActiveSessionThreshold is the maximum age of a session state file in an
   341→// ACTIVE phase before it is considered stale (crashed agent) and ignored by
   342→// hasAnyLiveSession. Only applies to ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED phases — an IDLE
   343→// session is always considered live regardless of age (user may just be away).
   344→const staleActiveSessionThreshold = 4 * time.Hour
   345→
   346→// hasAnyLiveSession checks if any session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE,
   347→// ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). Used to decide whether to defer review
   348→// application to the prompt-submit injection (visible to user) vs background
   349→// auto-apply (invisible).
   350→//
   351→// ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions older than staleActiveSessionThreshold are
   352→// skipped as likely orphaned from crashed processes. IDLE sessions are always
   353→// considered live regardless of age.
   354→func hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot string) bool {
   355→	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   356→	if sessDir == "" {
   357→		return false
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
   361→	if err != nil {
   362→		return false
   363→	}
   364→
   365→	const maxCheck = 50
   366→	checked := 0
   367→	for _, entry := range entries {
   368→		if checked >= maxCheck {
   369→			wingmanLog("stopping live-session scan after %d entries; assuming live session may exist", checked)
   370→			return true
   371→		}
   372→		name := entry.Name()
   373→		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   374→			continue
   375→		}
   376→		checked++
   377→
   378→		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
   379→		phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid)
   380→		if phase == "" || phase == string(session.PhaseEnded) {
   381→			continue
   382→		}
   383→
   384→		// ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions that haven't been updated in a
   385→		// long time are likely orphaned from crashed agents — skip them.
   386→		// IDLE sessions are always considered live (user may just be away).
   387→		if session.Phase(phase).IsActive() {
   388→			info, statErr := entry.Info()
   389→			if statErr != nil {
   390→				continue
   391→			}
   392→			if time.Since(info.ModTime()) > staleActiveSessionThreshold {
   393→				wingmanLog("skipping stale active session %s (phase=%s, age=%s)", sid, phase, time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   394→				continue
   395→			}
   396→		}

> AGENT

There it is again. `test.json` is still in `active_committed` phase with a modtime of 17:01 (11 minutes ago) — well within the 4h stale threshold. This phantom session state file blocks `hasAnyLiveSession` from returning false, so auto-apply never triggers.

The real issue is: `test.json` is a testing artifact that shouldn't exist. But more broadly, the system should be more robust — `hasAnyLiveSession` should consider the session ID naming pattern.

   676→		remaining := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx)
   677→
   678→		// Dispatch strategy-specific actions.
   679→		// Each branch handles its own BaseCommit update so there is no
   680→		// fallthrough conditional at the end. On condensation failure,
   681→		// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to

   683→		for _, action := range remaining {

   689→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible.
   690→				} else {
   691→					// No new content to condense — just update BaseCommit
   692→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   693→				}
   694→			case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
   695→				// The state machine already gates this action on HasFilesTouched,
   696→				// but hasNew is an additional content-level check (transcript has
   697→				// new content beyond what was previously condensed).
   698→				if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {
   699→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   700→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved (same as ActionCondense).
   701→				} else {

   703→				}
   704→			case session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   705→				if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   706→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",

   708→					)
   709→				}
   710→				s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   711→			case session.ActionMigrateShadowBranch:
   712→				// Deferred to pass 2 so condensation reads the old shadow branch first.
   713→				// Migration updates BaseCommit as part of the rename.
   714→				// Store checkpointID so HandleTurnEnd can reuse it for deferred condensation.
   715→				state.PendingCheckpointID = checkpointID.String()
   716→				pendingMigrations = append(pendingMigrations, pendingMigration{state: state})

   140→		return transitionFromActive(event, ctx)

   151→
   152→func transitionFromIdle(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   153→	switch event {
   154→	case EventTurnStart:
   155→		return TransitionResult{
   156→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   157→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   158→		}
   159→	case EventTurnEnd:
   160→		// Turn end while idle is a no-op (no active turn to end).
   161→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   162→	case EventGitCommit:
   163→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   164→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   165→		}
   166→		return TransitionResult{
   167→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   168→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   169→		}
   170→	case EventSessionStart:
   171→		// Already condensable, no-op.
   172→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   173→	case EventSessionStop:
   174→		return TransitionResult{
   175→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   176→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   177→		}
   178→	default:
   179→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   180→	}
   181→}
   182→
   183→func transitionFromActive(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   184→	switch event {
   185→	case EventTurnStart:
   186→		// Ctrl-C recovery: just continue.
   187→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   188→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   189→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   190→		return TransitionResult{
   191→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   192→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   193→		}
   194→	case EventTurnEnd:
   195→		return TransitionResult{
   196→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   197→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   198→		}
   199→	case EventGitCommit:
   200→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   201→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   202→		}
   203→		return TransitionResult{
   204→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   205→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   206→		}
   207→	case EventSessionStart:
   208→		return TransitionResult{
   209→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   210→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   211→		}
   212→	case EventSessionStop:
   213→		return TransitionResult{
   214→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   215→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   216→		}
   217→	default:
   218→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   219→	}
   220→}
   221→
   222→func transitionFromActiveCommitted(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   223→	switch event {
   224→	case EventTurnStart:
   225→		// Ctrl-C recovery after commit.
   226→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   227→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   228→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   229→		return TransitionResult{
   230→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   231→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   232→		}
   233→	case EventTurnEnd:
   234→		return TransitionResult{
   235→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   236→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   237→		}
   238→	case EventGitCommit:
   239→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   240→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   241→		}
   242→		return TransitionResult{
   243→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   244→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   245→		}
   246→	case EventSessionStart:
   247→		return TransitionResult{
   248→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   249→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   250→		}
   251→	case EventSessionStop:
   252→		return TransitionResult{
   253→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   254→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   255→		}
   256→	default:
   257→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   258→	}
   259→}
   260→
   261→func transitionFromEnded(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   262→	switch event {
   263→	case EventTurnStart:
   264→		return TransitionResult{
   265→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   266→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   267→		}
   268→	case EventTurnEnd:
   269→		// Turn end while ended is a no-op.
   270→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   271→	case EventGitCommit:
   272→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   273→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   274→		}
   275→		if ctx.HasFilesTouched {
   276→			return TransitionResult{
   277→				NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   278→				Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   279→			}
   280→		}
   281→		return TransitionResult{
   282→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   283→			Actions:  []Action{ActionDiscardIfNoFiles, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   284→		}
   285→	case EventSessionStart:
   286→		return TransitionResult{
   287→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   288→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt},
   289→		}
   290→	case EventSessionStop:
   291→		// Already ended, no-op.
   292→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   293→	default:
   294→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   295→	}
   296→}
   297→
   298→// ApplyCommonActions applies the common (non-strategy-specific) actions from a
   299→// TransitionResult to the given State. It updates Phase, LastInteractionTime,
   300→// and EndedAt as indicated by the transition.
   301→//
   302→// Returns the subset of actions that require strategy-specific handling

> AGENT

Now I understand the root cause. Here's what happens:

1. `PostCommit` processes ALL sessions in the worktree, including `test.json`
2. For `ACTIVE_COMMITTED + EventGitCommit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED`, the transition emits `ActionUpdateLastInteraction`
3. This refreshes `LastInteractionTime` in the JSON AND the file modtime (via `saveSessionState`)
4. So every commit made by the user keeps `test.json` looking "fresh", even though the agent that owned it is long dead
5. `hasAnyLiveSession` uses file modtime for staleness → never hits the 4h threshold

Two fixes needed:
1. Don't emit `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` for `ACTIVE_COMMITTED + GitCommit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED` (self-loop shouldn't refresh the clock)
2. `hasAnyLiveSession` should use `LastInteractionTime` from JSON, not file modtime (since file is always touched by save)

> TOOL

   223→	switch event {
   224→	case EventTurnStart:
   225→		// Ctrl-C recovery after commit.
   226→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   227→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   228→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   229→		return TransitionResult{
   230→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   231→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   232→		}
   233→	case EventTurnEnd:
   234→		return TransitionResult{
   235→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   236→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   237→		}
   238→	case EventGitCommit:
   239→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   240→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   241→		}

   243→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   244→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   245→		}
   246→	case EventSessionStart:
   247→		return TransitionResult{
   248→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   249→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   250→		}
   251→	case EventSessionStop:
   252→		return TransitionResult{
   253→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   254→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   255→		}
   256→	default:
   257→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   258→	}
   259→}

   346→// hasAnyLiveSession checks if any session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE,
   347→// ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). Used to decide whether to defer review
   348→// application to the prompt-submit injection (visible to user) vs background
   349→// auto-apply (invisible).
   350→//
   351→// ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions older than staleActiveSessionThreshold are
   352→// skipped as likely orphaned from crashed processes. IDLE sessions are always
   353→// considered live regardless of age.
   354→func hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot string) bool {
   355→	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   356→	if sessDir == "" {
   357→		return false
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
   361→	if err != nil {
   362→		return false
   363→	}
   364→
   365→	const maxCheck = 50
   366→	checked := 0
   367→	for _, entry := range entries {
   368→		if checked >= maxCheck {
   369→			wingmanLog("stopping live-session scan after %d entries; assuming live session may exist", checked)
   370→			return true
   371→		}
   372→		name := entry.Name()
   373→		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   374→			continue
   375→		}
   376→		checked++
   377→
   378→		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
   379→		phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid)
   380→		if phase == "" || phase == string(session.PhaseEnded) {
   381→			continue
   382→		}
   383→
   384→		// ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions that haven't been updated in a
   385→		// long time are likely orphaned from crashed agents — skip them.
   386→		// IDLE sessions are always considered live (user may just be away).
   387→		if session.Phase(phase).IsActive() {
   388→			info, statErr := entry.Info()
   389→			if statErr != nil {
   390→				continue
   391→			}
   392→			if time.Since(info.ModTime()) > staleActiveSessionThreshold {
   393→				wingmanLog("skipping stale active session %s (phase=%s, age=%s)", sid, phase, time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   394→				continue
   395→			}
   396→		}
   397→
   398→		wingmanLog("found live session %s (phase=%s)", sid, phase)
   399→		return true
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	return false
   403→}
   404→
   405→// findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by
```

}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go",
  "old_string": "func TestHasAnyLiveSession_StaleActiveSessionSkipped(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tsessDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".git\", \"entire-sessions\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(sessDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create an ACTIVE session file with a very old modification time\n\tsessFile := filepath.Join(sessDir, \"stale-active.json\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(sessFile, []byte(`{\"phase\":\"active\"}`), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\t// Set modification time beyond staleActiveSessionThreshold\n\tstaleTime := time.Now().Add(-staleActiveSessionThreshold - 1*time.Hour)\n\tif err := os.Chtimes(sessFile, staleTime, staleTime); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tif hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should return false when only active session is beyond staleActiveSessionThreshold\")\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "func TestHasAnyLiveSession_StaleActiveSessionSkipped(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tsessDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".git\", \"entire-sessions\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(sessDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create an ACTIVE session with last_interaction_time beyond threshold.\n\t// Uses JSON field (not file modtime) for staleness detection.\n\tstaleTime := time.Now().Add(-staleActiveSessionThreshold - 1*time.Hour)\n\tsessData := fmt.Sprintf(`{\"phase\":\"active\",\"last_interaction_time\":\"%s\"}`, staleTime.Format(time.RFC3339Nano))\n\tsessFile := filepath.Join(sessDir, \"stale-active.json\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(sessFile, []byte(sessData), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tif hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should return false when only active session is beyond staleActiveSessionThreshold\")\n\t}\n}"
}
```

}
